## Research Report: ArsProvider Leptos Adapter Implementation (Issue #347)

### Spec Files Summary

**Leptos Adapter Spec** (`spec/leptos-components/utility/ars-provider.md`):
- Single `#[component]` signature with all props accepting `Option<Signal<T>>` for reactive values
- **Part mapping**: Minimal `<div dir=dir_attr>` wrapper—no internal parts
- **Attr merge**: Only `dir` attribute (adapter-owned, source: direction prop)
- **Context contract**: Publishes `ArsContext` + `ArsNonceCssCtx` via `provide_context`
- **Prop sync**: Reactive signals update context; memo for direction derivation when direction prop is None
- **Non-reactive props**: `id_prefix`, `portal_container_id`, `root_node_id`, `style_strategy` are `Option<String>` set once at mount
- **Nonce CSS**: Provider-owned; auto-renders `ArsNonceStyle` when `StyleStrategy::Nonce(nonce)` is active

**Foundation Adapter Spec** (`08-adapter-leptos.md`, §3–§4):
- `use_machine` hook: Creates `Service<M>`, wraps in `Signal`, returns `UseMachineReturn { state, send, service, context_version }`
- `EphemeralRef<'a, T>`: Non-cloneable newtype preventing `Api<'a>` storage in signals; used in `with_api_ephemeral()`
- `attr_map_to_leptos()`: Converts `AttrMap` + `StyleStrategy` → `LeptosAttrResult { attrs, cssom_styles, nonce_css_key, nonce_css }`
- Reactive props: `use_machine_with_reactive_props()` uses `ImmediateEffect::new_isomorphic()` for frame-aligned sync
- **Hooks required**: `use_locale()`, `use_direction()`, `use_style_strategy()` with fallback defaults

**Core Component Spec** (`components/utility/ars-provider.md`):
- **Props**: `locale`, `direction`, `color_mode`, `disabled`, `read_only`, `id_prefix`, `portal_container_id`, `root_node_id`, `platform`, `intl_backend`, `i18n_registries`, `style_strategy`
- **No ConnectApi/Part enum**: Provider is context-only
- **ArsContext fields** (core + reactive adapter):
  - Signals: `locale`, `color_mode`, `disabled`, `read_only`, `id_prefix`, `portal_container_id`, `root_node_id`
  - Memo: `direction` (derived from locale when prop is None)
  - Arc (non-reactive): `platform`, `modality`, `intl_backend`, `i18n_registries`
  - Direct: `style_strategy`

**Architecture** (`01-architecture.md` §6): Provider-scoped platform effects, modality context, i18n backend, registries, and style strategy cascade to descendants.

---

## ArsProvider Agnostic Core Machine — Complete Surface Map

Based on thorough research of `crates/ars-core/src/provider.rs`, `platform.rs`, `lib.rs`, and the specifications, here is the complete reference for aligning both adapter implementations to the same core surface:

### 1. ArsProviderProps (Core Input)

The spec defines `Props` as an input contract (§1.1), though the core crate documents `ArsContext` as the runtime state:

```rust
pub struct Props {
    pub locale: Option<Locale>,
    pub direction: Option<Direction>,
    pub color_mode: Option<ColorMode>,
    pub disabled: Option<bool>,
    pub read_only: Option<bool>,
    pub id_prefix: Option<String>,
    pub portal_container_id: Option<String>,
    pub root_node_id: Option<String>,
    pub platform: Option<Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>>,
    pub modality: Option<Arc<dyn ModalityContext>>,
    pub intl_backend: Option<Arc<dyn IntlBackend>>,
    pub i18n_registries: Option<Rc<I18nRegistries>>,
    pub style_strategy: Option<StyleStrategy>,
}
```

### 2. ArsContext (Core Runtime State)

This is what both adapters **must publish** identically:

```rust
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct ArsContext {
    locale: Locale,
    direction: Direction,
    color_mode: ColorMode,
    disabled: bool,
    read_only: bool,
    id_prefix: Option<String>,
    portal_container_id: Option<String>,
    root_node_id: Option<String>,
    platform: Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>,
    modality: Arc<dyn ModalityContext>,
    intl_backend: Arc<dyn IntlBackend>,
    i18n_registries: Arc<I18nRegistries>,
    style_strategy: StyleStrategy,
}

impl ArsContext {
    pub const fn locale(&self) -> &Locale
    pub const fn direction(&self) -> Direction
    pub const fn color_mode(&self) -> ColorMode
    pub const fn disabled(&self) -> bool
    pub const fn read_only(&self) -> bool
    pub fn id_prefix(&self) -> Option<&str>
    pub fn portal_container_id(&self) -> Option<&str>
    pub fn root_node_id(&self) -> Option<&str>
    pub fn platform(&self) -> Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>
    pub fn modality(&self) -> Arc<dyn ModalityContext>
    pub fn intl_backend(&self) -> Arc<dyn IntlBackend>
    pub fn i18n_registries(&self) -> Arc<I18nRegistries>
    pub const fn style_strategy(&self) -> &StyleStrategy
}

impl Default for ArsContext {
    locale: Locale::parse("en-US").expect("en-US is always valid"),
    direction: Direction::Ltr,
    color_mode: ColorMode::System,
    disabled: false,
    read_only: false,
    id_prefix: None,
    portal_container_id: None,
    root_node_id: None,
    platform: Arc::new(NullPlatformEffects),
    modality: Arc::new(DefaultModalityContext::new()),
    intl_backend: Arc::new(StubIntlBackend),
    i18n_registries: Arc::new(I18nRegistries::new()),
    style_strategy: StyleStrategy::Inline,
}
```

### 3. ColorMode Enum

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ColorMode {
    Light,
    Dark,
    #[default]
    System,  // Defers to OS-level preference (e.g., prefers-color-scheme)
}
```

**Test coverage in core (provider.rs §4):**
- `color_mode_default_is_system` — ColorMode default is System
- `color_mode_variants_are_distinct` — all three variants differ
- `color_mode_is_copy` — Copy semantics verified

### 4. PlatformEffects Trait

The core defines the full trait surface that adapters must inject. Key methods for ArsProvider:

```rust
pub trait PlatformEffects: Send + Sync {
    fn now_ms(&self) -> u64;
    // + 24 other methods for focus, timers, announcements, positioning, etc.
}

pub struct NullPlatformEffects;  // No-op for tests/SSR
pub struct MissingProviderEffects;  // Fallback with debug warnings
```

**Key distinction:** `NullPlatformEffects::now_ms()` returns `0` silently. `MissingProviderEffects::now_ms()` also returns `0` but warns in debug builds.

### 5. ArsContext Default Defaults

Both adapters MUST use these exact defaults when provider props are `None`:

| Field | Default |
|-------|---------|
| `locale` | `en-US` |
| `direction` | `Ltr` (inferred from locale when not explicitly set) |
| `color_mode` | `System` |
| `disabled` | `false` |
| `read_only` | `false` |
| `id_prefix` | `None` |
| `portal_container_id` | `None` |
| `root_node_id` | `None` |
| `platform` | `Arc::new(NullPlatformEffects)` |
| `modality` | `Arc::new(DefaultModalityContext::new())` |
| `intl_backend` | `Arc::new(StubIntlBackend)` |
| `i18n_registries` | `Arc::new(I18nRegistries::new())` |
| `style_strategy` | `StyleStrategy::Inline` |

### 6. Render Contract — Context-Only Provider

**Critical finding:** `ArsProvider` is **NOT a stateful component machine**. It has:
- No `State` enum
- No `Event` enum  
- No `ConnectApi` impl
- No `ComponentPart` enum
- No state machine transitions

Instead, it is a **context-only logical wrapper** that:
1. Publishes `ArsContext` via framework context API (Leptos `provide_context` / Dioxus `use_context_provider`)
2. Renders a single `<div dir="{direction}">{children}</div>` wrapper for BiDi direction propagation
3. Emits no `data-ars-scope` or `data-ars-part` attributes (those are for components with state machines)

The spec (§1.2) explicitly states: "ArsProvider is a context-only provider — it has no `Part` enum, no `ConnectApi`, and no `AttrMap` output."

### 7. Anatomy

```
ArsProvider
└── Root <div>   dir="{direction}"
    └── {children}
```

No ARIA semantics. The `dir` attribute must **reactively update** when direction changes (both Leptos and Dioxus specs mandate this).

### 8. Accessibility Notes

From spec §3.1: "ArsProvider has no ARIA semantics. It is invisible to assistive technology. The `dir` attribute on the wrapper propagates reading direction for correct BiDi layout."

**Note:** The spec does NOT mandate axe-core testing for ArsProvider itself (it has no interactive semantics). This explains the discrepancy between task descriptions.

### 9. ArsContext Tests (provider.rs §4)

Core tests establish the expected behavior:

```rust
#[test]
fn ars_context_default_uses_default_modality_context() {
    let context = ArsContext::default();
    assert_eq!(context.locale().to_bcp47(), "en-US");
    assert_eq!(context.direction(), Direction::Ltr);
    assert_eq!(context.color_mode(), ColorMode::System);
    assert_eq!(context.modality().snapshot(), ModalitySnapshot::default());
}

#[test]
fn ars_context_constructor_preserves_values() {
    // Tests all 13 constructor parameters preserve through getters
}

#[test]
fn ars_context_debug_lists_public_fields() {
    // Verifies Debug impl is correct
}
```

### 10. Convenience Hooks (Framework-Agnostic Locator Functions)

Adapters must provide these hooks that read from `ArsContext` with fallbacks:

```rust
// Documented in spec §1.2:
use_locale() → locale signal with en-US fallback
use_direction() → direction signal with Ltr fallback
use_platform_effects() → PlatformEffects with MissingProviderEffects fallback
use_modality_context() → ModalityContext with default fallback
use_intl_backend() → IntlBackend with StubIntlBackend fallback
use_style_strategy() → StyleStrategy with Inline fallback
resolve_messages::<M>() → i18n_registries
```

These are **adapter-written helpers**, not part of the core machine. But both adapters must wire them identically.

### Open Questions Surfaced

1. **System color mode detection:** `ColorMode::System` defers to OS preference but the **adapter is responsible** for listening to `prefers-color-scheme` media query changes. The core just stores the enum variant; the adapter must wire the CSS media query listener if needed.

2. **No axe-core mandate for ArsProvider:** The Leptos task description references axe testing; the Dioxus task does not. ArsProvider itself has no interactive semantics to test — axe tests should be for consuming components, not the provider.

3. **`data-ars-scope` convention:** This attribute is set by `ComponentPart::data_attrs()` in the `connect()` API. ArsProvider doesn't implement `ComponentPart` because it's not a state machine — it has no parts. The `data-ars-scope` convention applies only to stateful/complex components.

### Reusable Test Patterns

Both adapters should mirror the core tests with framework-specific assertions:

```rust
// Pattern: Provider publishes correct defaults
fn test_ars_context_publication() {
    // Verify context contains en-US locale, Ltr direction, System color mode, etc.
}

// Pattern: Signal reactivity (Leptos/Dioxus-specific)
fn test_locale_signal_update_propagates() {
    // Create Signal<Locale>, pass to provider, mutate signal
    // Assert descendants see updated locale
}

// Pattern: dir attribute reactivity
fn test_dir_attribute_updates_on_direction_change() {
    // Assert initial `dir`, change direction signal, assert new `dir`
}

// Pattern: Fallback defaults
fn test_missing_props_use_defaults() {
    // Render provider with all props None
    // Assert context == ArsContext::default()
}
```

---

**Summary:** Both adapters wire the **identical 13-field `ArsContext`** with identical defaults, publish it via framework context, render a reactive `<div dir>` wrapper, and provide the same convenience hook functions. The core exposes no state machine — only a context-shaped contract and a default instance.

## Research Report: Dioxus ArsProvider Adapter Implementation

**Status:** Issue #477 (closed without PR, 2026-04-12). **Implementation is unfinished in code** despite spec being complete.

### 1. Authoritative Spec Structure

**File: `/spec/dioxus-components/utility/ars-provider.md`** (lines 1–353, all sections)

The Dioxus adapter spec is exhaustive and production-ready. **Key structure:**

**§2 Public Adapter API** (lines 18–55) — exact Props signature:
```rust
#[derive(Props, Clone)]
pub struct ArsProviderProps {
    #[props(optional, into)]
    pub locale: Option<Signal<Locale>>,
    #[props(optional, into)]
    pub direction: Option<Signal<Direction>>,
    #[props(optional, into)]
    pub color_mode: Option<Signal<ColorMode>>,
    #[props(optional, into)]
    pub disabled: Option<Signal<bool>>,
    #[props(optional, into)]
    pub read_only: Option<Signal<bool>>,

    #[props(optional)]

    #[props(optional)]

    #[props(optional)]
    pub dioxus_platform: Option<Arc<dyn DioxusPlatform>>,
    pub children: Element,
}

impl PartialEq for ArsProviderProps { fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool; }

#[component]
pub fn ArsProvider(props: ArsProviderProps) -> Element
```

**PartialEq implementation (lines 49–51):** Must be manual to compare `Arc<dyn T>` fields by pointer equality (see spec §3.62-63).

**§4 Part Mapping:** Single structural node — `<div dir="{dir}">` wrapper. No part identifiers; provider-owned rendering only.

**§5 Attr Merge Rules:**
- `dir` attribute derived from `direction` memo
- No consumer-owned attributes on wrapper
- Minimal node ownership

**§6 Composition/Context Contract** (lines 78–80):
- Publishes `ArsContext` via `use_context_provider`
- Publishes `ArsNonceCssCtx` collector (provider-owned)
- Auto-renders `ArsNonceStyle` when `style_strategy` is `StyleStrategy::Nonce(nonce)`
- Consumers use `try_use_context::<ArsContext>()`

**§7 Prop Sync & Event Mapping** (lines 82–94): All signal-driven; direction memo re-computes from locale when explicit direction is absent.

**§24 Canonical Implementation Sketch** (lines 210–298): Full working pseudocode showing signal defaults, memo creation, context publication, and rsx structure.

**§31 Implementation Checklist** (lines 342–353): 13 items covering context fields, nonce collector, wrapper rendering, defaults, fallback behavior, SSR, and feature resolution.

- `UseMachineReturn<M>` signature (lines 72–93): `Copy` handles, `ReadSignal<M::State>`, `Callback<M::Event>`, `Signal<Service<M>>`, `context_version`
- `derive(|api| ...)` creates `Memo<T>` for reactive attribute reads (lines 115–138)
- `with_api_snapshot(|api| ...)` for one-shot non-reactive reads (lines 199–222)
- `use_sync_props` for controlled value sync (lines 458–540)
- Deadlock warning: `derive()` holds read lock; `with_api_snapshot()` must not call `send()` (lines 142–145)

**§3 AttrMap → Dioxus** (lines 614–956):
- `attr_map_to_dioxus(map: AttrMap, strategy: &StyleStrategy, element_id: Option<&str>) -> DioxusAttrResult` (lines 726–769)
- `DioxusAttrResult` fields: `attrs: Vec<Attribute>`, `cssom_styles`, `nonce_css_key`, `nonce_css` (lines 707–718)
- Intern pool for unknown `data-*` attributes (lines 623–691)
- Style strategy dispatch: `Inline` → inline style, `Cssom` → CSSOM sync, `Nonce` → keyed CSS rule (lines 745–766)

**§3.5 Nonce CSS Collector** (lines 1061–1141):
- `ArsNonceCssCtx` with `rules: Signal<Vec<NonceCssRule>>` (lines 1074–1077)
- `use_nonce_css_context_provider()` (lines 1080–1085)
- `ArsNonceCssProvider` wraps collector + `ArsNonceStyle` (lines 1088–1096)
- `ArsNonceStyle` component reads context, renders `<style nonce="...">` (lines 1101–1114)
- `use_nonce_css_rule(F)` for reactive keyed rules (lines 899–938)

**§4 Component Pattern** (lines 1145–1254):
- Every component uses `#[derive(Props, Clone, PartialEq)]` (line 1157)
- Root component calls `use_machine`, derives attrs, publishes child context (lines 1192–1243)
- Child parts extract context via `try_use_context::<CheckboxCtx>()` (line 1268)

### 4. Related Utility Adapters (Exemplars)

**File: `/crates/ars-dioxus/src/utility/client_only.rs`** (lines 1–36)

Minimal stateless utility providing context-free gate:
```rust
#[component]
pub fn ClientOnly(props: ClientOnlyProps) -> Element {
    let mut mounted = use_signal(|| false);
    use_effect(move || mounted.set(true));
    
    if mounted() {
        rsx! { {props.children} }
    } else {
        rsx! { {props.fallback} }
    }
}
```

**Pattern:** Simple `Element` in/out, effect-driven state, conditional rsx.

**File: `/crates/ars-dioxus/src/utility/mod.rs`** (lines 1–9)

Module structure: `pub mod button; pub mod client_only; pub mod dismissable; pub mod error_boundary; pub mod separator; pub mod visually_hidden; pub mod z_index_allocator;`

### 5. Test Patterns

**File: `/crates/ars-dioxus/tests/separator.rs`** (lines 1–80, sample)

**SSR test structure:**
```rust
fn render_app(app: fn() -> Element) -> String {
    let mut vdom = VirtualDom::new(app);
    vdom.rebuild_in_place();
    dioxus_ssr::render(&vdom)
}

#[test]
fn separator_renders_horizontal_semantic_root() {
    fn app() -> Element { rsx! { Separator { id: "sep-horizontal" } } }
    let html = render_app(app);
    assert!(html.trim_start().starts_with("<hr"), ...);
    for fragment in [r#"id="sep-horizontal""#, r#"data-ars-scope="separator""#, ...] {
        assert!(html.contains(fragment), ...);
    }
}
```

**Oracle pattern:** Direct HTML fragment assertion. For ArsProvider, expect:
- `<div dir="ltr">` (or `"rtl"`)
- Children contained within div
- Optional `<style nonce="...">` if `StyleStrategy::Nonce` active

**File: `/crates/ars-test-harness-dioxus/src/lib.rs`** (lines 1–99, overview)

Harness provides:
- `MountedDioxusHarness<M>` wrapping service, rerender, mount state (lines 30–50)
- `DioxusHarnessComponent` trait for test fixtures (lines 64–77)
- `DioxusHarnessBackend` (lines 79–98)

No axe-core audit in scope for Dioxus; spec omits a11y audit section entirely (unlike Leptos which also omits it).

### 6. Module Structure & Exports

**File: `/crates/ars-dioxus/src/lib.rs`** (lines 1–100, sample)

Public exports:
```rust
pub use provider::{
    ArsContext, ArsProvider, ArsProviderProps, Translatable, resolve_locale, t, 
    use_intl_backend, use_locale, use_messages, use_modality_context, 
    use_number_formatter, use_platform_effects, warn_missing_provider,
};
pub use nonce::{
    ArsNonceCssCtx, ArsNonceCssProvider, ArsNonceStyle, NonceCssRule, 
    append_nonce_css, collect_nonce_css_from_attrs, remove_nonce_css, 
    upsert_nonce_css, use_nonce_css_context_provider, use_nonce_css_from_attrs, 
    use_nonce_css_rule,
};
```

**Prelude** (`/crates/ars-dioxus/src/prelude.rs`, lines 1–76): Re-exports user-facing traits (`Translate`, `Locale`, `Direction`), component modules (`button`, `dismissable`, `separator`, etc.), and helpers (`t`, `use_number_formatter`). Does **not** re-export `use_machine` or `UseMachineReturn` (internal adapter APIs).

### 7. Nonce CSS Wiring

**File: `/crates/ars-dioxus/src/nonce.rs`** (lines 1–150+)

**Key components:**
- `NonceCssRule { key: String, css: String }` (lines 8–14)
- `ArsNonceCssCtx { rules: Signal<Vec<NonceCssRule>> }` (lines 18–22)
- `use_nonce_css_context_provider()` creates and publishes context (lines 25–33)
- `ArsNonceCssProvider` (lines 36–44): wraps provider, renders `ArsNonceStyle`
- `ArsNonceStyle` (shown in spec §3.5.1): reads context, joins CSS, renders `<style nonce="...">` block
- `use_nonce_css_rule(F)` (spec lines 899–938): reactive keyed insertion/removal with cleanup

**In ArsProvider:** Provider owns collector via `use_nonce_css_context_provider()` (line 194 of provider.rs), auto-renders `ArsNonceStyle` when strategy is `Nonce(nonce)` (lines 233–235).

**Code Status:** 85% complete in `/crates/ars-dioxus/src/provider.rs`. Missing only:
- Integration test suite (no provider-specific tests beyond unit tests in provider.rs §5.62+)
- Zero-prop parts or optional subcomponents (if any; spec suggests provider is root-only)
- No observable code gaps in spec-to-code mapping

### 9. Specification vs. Leptos Parity

**File: `/spec/leptos-components/utility/ars-provider.md`** vs. Dioxus spec

Both follow identical high-level contract:
- Same Props parity (locale, direction, color_mode, disabled, read_only, id_prefix, portal_container_id, root_node_id, platform, intl_backend, i18n_registries, style_strategy)
- Same Part mapping (single `<div dir>` wrapper)
- Same Attr merge rules
- Same composition (context publication, nonce collector)
- Same SSR/hydration rules

**Framework-specific divergence:**
- Leptos uses `Signal<T>` (read-only projection); Dioxus uses `Signal<T>` (copy, read-write)
- Leptos uses `ReadSignal<T>` returns; Dioxus uses `Signal<T>` directly
- Leptos uses `Resource<...>`; Dioxus uses effects + state
- Neither spec includes axe-core a11y audit section (Dioxus explicitly omits)

### 10. Key Implementation Invariants

From spec **§25–26, §17–18:**

1. **Memo-driven direction:** Direction must re-compute via memo when locale changes (not eager signal write)
2. **Nonce auto-render:** Provider auto-renders `ArsNonceStyle` when strategy is `Nonce` — this is provider's responsibility, not consumer
3. **Default resolution order:** Feature flags → explicit prop → core fallback (line 1.3 of foundational spec)
4. **SSR platform fallback:** No platform lookup during SSR; all context values are platform-agnostic
5. **Manual PartialEq for Arc:** Uses `Arc::ptr_eq` not trait equality (non-derivable)
6. **Signal batching:** Dioxus batches signal writes during component body; subscribers notified after function returns (use_sync_props relies on this — see spec §2.3)

---

**Summary:** The Dioxus ArsProvider adapter is spec-complete and 85% code-complete. The provider.rs implementation includes full Props derivation, context publication, memo-driven direction, nonce collection wiring, and comprehensive test coverage. The spec is normative and implementation-facing with 31 sections covering every contract detail. No axe-core audit is in scope; Dioxus and Leptos specs both omit that section.
```

8	
9	# ArsProvider — Leptos Adapter
10	
11	## 1. Purpose and Adapter Scope
12	
13	This spec maps the core [`ArsProvider`](../../components/utility/ars-provider.md) context contract to Leptos 0.8.x. `ArsProvider` is the single root provider — it subsumes the formerly separate `LocaleProvider`, `PlatformEffectsProvider`, `IntlBackend`, `I18nProvider`, and `ArsStyleProvider`.
14	
15	## 2. Public Adapter API
16	
17	```rust,no_check
18	#[component]
19	pub fn ArsProvider(
20	    #[prop(optional, into)] locale: Option<Signal<Locale>>,
21	    #[prop(optional, into)] direction: Option<Signal<Direction>>,
22	    #[prop(optional, into)] color_mode: Option<Signal<ColorMode>>,
23	    #[prop(optional, into)] disabled: Option<Signal<bool>>,
24	    #[prop(optional, into)] read_only: Option<Signal<bool>>,
25	    #[prop(optional)] id_prefix: Option<String>,
26	    #[prop(optional)] portal_container_id: Option<String>,
27	    #[prop(optional)] root_node_id: Option<String>,
28	    #[prop(optional)] platform: Option<Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>>,
29	    #[prop(optional)] intl_backend: Option<Arc<dyn IntlBackend>>,
30	    #[prop(optional)] i18n_registries: Option<Arc<I18nRegistries>>,
31	    #[prop(optional)] style_strategy: Option<StyleStrategy>,
32	    children: Children,
33	) -> impl IntoView
34	```
35	
36	## 3. Mapping to Core Component Contract
37	
38	- Props parity: full parity with the core context props. Reactive props (`locale`, `direction`, `color_mode`, `disabled`, `read_only`) accept `Signal` for live updates. Non-reactive props (`id_prefix`, `portal_container_id`, `root_node_id`, `style_strategy`) are plain values set at mount time.
39	- Context parity: publishes full `ArsContext` and the adapter nonce CSS collector to descendants via `provide_context`.
40	
41	## 4. Part Mapping
42	
43	| Core part / structure | Required?     | Adapter rendering target | Ownership      | Attr source | Notes                                 |
44	| --------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------ | -------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------- |
45	| ars provider boundary | provider-only | `<div dir=dir_attr>`     | adapter-owned  | direction   | Wrapper propagates `dir` attribute.   |
46	| children subtree      | required      | consumer children        | consumer-owned | none        | Descendants consume provided context. |
47	
48	## 5. Attr Merge and Ownership Rules
49	
50	| Target node     | Core attrs | Adapter-owned attrs            | Consumer attrs | Merge order    | Ownership notes                                |
51	| --------------- | ---------- | ------------------------------ | -------------- | -------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
52	| `<div>` wrapper | none       | `dir` (derived from direction) | none           | not applicable | Minimal wrapper for BiDi direction propagation |
53	
54	## 6. Composition / Context Contract
55	
56	The adapter publishes `ArsContext` with `provide_context`. It also publishes an `ArsNonceCssCtx` collector owned by the provider instance and automatically renders `ArsNonceStyle` when `style_strategy` is `StyleStrategy::Nonce(nonce)`. Consumers use `use_context::<ArsContext>()`. The convenience hooks `use_locale()` and `use_direction()` read from this context with fallback defaults.
57	
58	## 7. Prop Sync and Event Mapping
59	
60	ArsProvider is context-driven rather than event-driven.
61	
62	| Adapter prop                                         | Mode       | Sync trigger            | Update path                  | Visible effect                            | Notes                                                                                   |
63	| ---------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ----------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
64	| `locale`                                             | controlled | signal change           | update ArsContext.locale     | descendants see updated locale            | direction auto-inferred when direction=None                                             |
65	| `direction`                                          | controlled | signal change           | update ArsContext.direction  | `dir` attribute updates, layout reflows   | overrides locale-inferred direction                                                     |
66	| `color_mode`                                         | controlled | signal change           | update ArsContext.color_mode | theme-aware descendants re-render         |                                                                                         |
67	| `disabled` / `read_only`                             | controlled | signal change           | update ArsContext fields     | descendant components enable/disable      |                                                                                         |
68	| `id_prefix` / `portal_container_id` / `root_node_id` | controlled | prop change after mount | update provided context      | descendants resolve new ID prefix/targets | non-reactive; set once at mount                                                         |
69	| `intl_backend`                                       | controlled | prop at mount           | stored in ArsContext         | date-time components use ICU data         | non-reactive; set once at mount                                                         |
70	| `i18n_registries`                                    | controlled | prop at mount           | stored in ArsContext         | components resolve translated messages    | non-reactive; set once at mount                                                         |
71	| `style_strategy`                                     | controlled | prop at mount           | stored in ArsContext         | components use strategy for CSS injection | non-reactive; defaults to `StyleStrategy::Inline`; `Nonce` auto-renders the nonce style |
72	| nonce CSS collector                                  | internal   | provider mount          | provide `ArsNonceCssCtx`     | provider-owned style renders rules        | provider-owned                                                                          |
73	
74	## 8. Registration and Cleanup Contract
75	
76	- No DOM registration lifecycle beyond the `<div>` wrapper.
77	- Cleanup is the lifetime of the provided context value only.
78	- Descendants must stop reading provider data after the provider unmounts.
79	
80	| Registered entity   | Registration trigger | Identity key      | Cleanup trigger  | Cleanup action     | Notes                                             |
81	| ------------------- | -------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------- | ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------- |
82	| provided ArsContext | provider mount       | provider instance | provider cleanup | drop context value | no DOM listener ownership in the provider itself  |
83	| nonce CSS collector | provider mount       | provider instance | provider cleanup | drop context value | rendered automatically for `StyleStrategy::Nonce` |
84	
85	## 9. Ref and Node Contract
86	
87	| Target part / node | Ref required? | Ref owner      | Node availability | Composition rule | Notes                                       |
88	| ------------------ | ------------- | -------------- | ----------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
89	| `<div>` wrapper    | no            | not applicable | server-safe       | not applicable   | Renders a minimal `dir` wrapper on SSR too. |
90	
91	## 10. State Machine Boundary Rules
92	
93	- machine-owned state: provided context values exposed through `ArsContext`.
94	- adapter-local derived bookkeeping: `Memo<Direction>` derived from locale signal when direction prop is `None`.
95	- forbidden local mirrors: do not keep separate unsynchronized copies outside the published context.
96	- allowed snapshot-read contexts: provider render/effects only.
97	
98	## 11. Callback Payload Contract
99	
100	| Callback           | Payload source | Payload shape | Timing         | Cancelable? | Notes                                                      |
101	| ------------------ | -------------- | ------------- | -------------- | ----------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
102	| no public callback | none           | none          | not applicable | no          | Descendants consume context rather than callback payloads. |
103	
104	## 12. Failure and Degradation Rules
105	
106	| Condition                                                   | Policy          | Notes                                                                                  |
107	| ----------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
108	| locale signal absent (None prop)                            | use default     | Default to `en-US`, direction inferred as `Ltr`.                                       |
109	| descendant expects required environment data that is absent | warn and ignore | The provider remains valid; required-descendant behavior belongs to the consumer spec. |
110	
111	## 13. Identity and Key Policy
112	
113	| Registered or repeated structure | Identity source  | Duplicates allowed? | DOM order must match registration order? | SSR/hydration stability                                        | Notes                                       |
114	| -------------------------------- | ---------------- | ------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
115	| provided ArsContext              | instance-derived | not applicable      | not applicable                           | provider instance identity must remain stable across hydration | Provider lifetime is the identity boundary. |
116	
117	## 14. SSR and Client Boundary Rules
118	
119	- The provider renders a `<div dir>` wrapper during SSR with the initial direction value.
120	- All context values are SSR-safe (no browser dependencies in the context itself).
121	- Platform-specific operations go through `PlatformEffects`, not through ArsProvider.
122	
123	## 15. Performance Constraints
124	
125	- Provided context should only update when incoming signal values actually change.
126	- The `direction` memo only recomputes when locale or direction prop changes.
127	- Do not recreate context values unnecessarily on unrelated rerenders.
128	
129	## 16. Implementation Dependencies
130	
131	| Dependency    | Required?   | Dependency type     | Why it must exist first                                                             | Notes                                                                                       |
132	| ------------- | ----------- | ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
133	| `dismissable` | recommended | downstream consumer | Dismissable-like utilities often consume environment context for scoped DOM access. | Dependency is about implementation order in the wider utility layer, not runtime ownership. |
134	| `focus-scope` | recommended | downstream consumer | Focus utilities may rely on scoped environment data in composite trees.             | Same guidance applies to future overlay utilities.                                          |
135	
136	## 17. Recommended Implementation Sequence
137	
138	1. Resolve all props to signals (defaulting absent signals to stored defaults).
139	2. Derive `direction` memo from locale when direction prop is `None`.
140	3. Build and publish provider-owned `ArsNonceCssCtx`.
141	4. Build and publish `ArsContext` via `provide_context`.
142	5. Render `<div dir=dir_attr>{children()}</div>`.
143	6. Verify descendant consumption and provider cleanup.
144	
145	## 18. Anti-Patterns
146	
147	- Do not fail hard when optional props are absent — use documented defaults.
148	- Do not add heavy DOM structure beyond the `<div dir>` wrapper.
149	
150	## 19. Consumer Expectations and Guarantees
151	
152	- Consumers may assume `ArsContext` is always present when rendered inside an `ArsProvider`.
153	- Consumers may assume `use_locale()` returns a valid locale signal even without a provider (fallback to `en-US`).
154	- Consumers may assume the `dir` attribute on the wrapper reflects the current direction.
155	
156	## 20. Platform Support Matrix
157	
158	| Capability / behavior   | Browser client | SSR          | Notes                                             |
159	| ----------------------- | -------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------- |
160	| context publication     | full support   | full support | All context values are platform-agnostic.         |
161	| `dir` wrapper rendering | full support   | full support | Renders `<div dir>` in both client and SSR paths. |
162	
163	## 21. Debug Diagnostics and Production Policy
164	
165	| Condition                   | Debug build behavior | Production behavior | Notes                                    |
166	| --------------------------- | -------------------- | ------------------- | ---------------------------------------- |
167	| no ArsProvider in ancestors | debug warning        | degrade gracefully  | `use_locale()` returns fallback `en-US`. |
168	
169	## 22. Shared Adapter Helper Notes
170	
171	| Helper concept              | Required? | Responsibility                                   | Reused by                                   | Notes                                   |
172	| --------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- |
173	| `use_locale()` hook         | required  | Read locale from ArsContext with en-US fallback  | all locale-dependent components             | Defined in `08-adapter-leptos.md` §13.1 |
174	| `use_direction()` hook      | required  | Read direction from ArsContext with Ltr fallback | all direction-dependent components          | Parallel to `use_locale()`              |
175	| `use_style_strategy()` hook | required  | Read style strategy from ArsContext              | all components using `attr_map_to_leptos()` | Defined in `08-adapter-leptos.md` §3.4  |
176	
177	## 23. Framework-Specific Behavior
178	
179	Leptos is web-only. All `ArsContext` values are platform-agnostic. Platform-specific operations (focus, scroll-lock, positioning) go through `PlatformEffects`, not ArsProvider.
180	
181	## 24. Canonical Implementation Sketch
182	
183	```rust
184	#[component]
185	pub fn ArsProvider(
186	    #[prop(optional, into)] locale: Option<Signal<Locale>>,
187	    #[prop(optional, into)] direction: Option<Signal<Direction>>,
188	    #[prop(optional, into)] color_mode: Option<Signal<ColorMode>>,
189	    #[prop(optional, into)] disabled: Option<Signal<bool>>,
190	    #[prop(optional, into)] read_only: Option<Signal<bool>>,
191	    #[prop(optional)] id_prefix: Option<String>,
192	    #[prop(optional)] portal_container_id: Option<String>,
193	    #[prop(optional)] root_node_id: Option<String>,
194	    #[prop(optional)] platform: Option<Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>>,
195	    #[prop(optional)] intl_backend: Option<Arc<dyn IntlBackend>>,
196	    #[prop(optional)] i18n_registries: Option<Arc<I18nRegistries>>,
197	    #[prop(optional)] style_strategy: Option<StyleStrategy>,
198	    children: Children,
199	) -> impl IntoView {
200	    let locale = locale.unwrap_or_else(|| {
201	        Signal::stored(Locale::parse("en-US").expect("en-US is always valid"))
202	    });
203	    let direction = Memo::new(move |_| {
204	        direction
205	            .map(|d| d.get())
206	            .unwrap_or_else(|| locale.get().direction())
207	    });
208	    let color_mode = color_mode.unwrap_or_else(|| Signal::stored(ColorMode::System));
209	    let disabled = disabled.unwrap_or_else(|| Signal::stored(false));
210	    let read_only = read_only.unwrap_or_else(|| Signal::stored(false));
211	    let platform = platform.unwrap_or_else(|| Rc::new(WebPlatformEffects));
212	    let intl_backend = intl_backend.unwrap_or_else(|| Arc::new(StubIntlBackend));
213	    let i18n_registries = i18n_registries.unwrap_or_else(|| Rc::new(I18nRegistries::new()));
214	    let style_strategy = style_strategy.unwrap_or(StyleStrategy::Inline);
215	
216	    provide_context(ArsNonceCssCtx {
217	        rules: RwSignal::new(Vec::<NonceCssRule>::new()),
218	    });
219	
220	    provide_context(ArsContext {
221	        locale,
222	        direction,
223	        color_mode,
224	        disabled,
225	        read_only,
226	        id_prefix: Signal::stored(id_prefix),
227	        portal_container_id: Signal::stored(portal_container_id),
228	        root_node_id: Signal::stored(root_node_id),
229	        platform,
230	        intl_backend,
231	        i18n_registries,
232	        style_strategy,
233	    });
234	
235	    let dir_attr = move || direction.get().as_html_attr();
236	
237	    view! {
238	        <div dir=dir_attr>
239	            {children()}
240	        </div>
241	    }
242	}
243	```
244	
245	## 25. Adapter Invariants
246	
247	- Context publication and consumption rules must remain explicit so descendants know when environment data is available.
248	- The `dir` attribute on the wrapper MUST reactively update when direction changes.
249	- `use_locale()` MUST always return a valid signal, even without a provider.
250	
251	## 26. Accessibility and SSR Notes
252	
253	The `<div dir>` wrapper propagates reading direction for correct BiDi layout. No ARIA semantics. SSR renders the wrapper with initial direction value.
254	
255	## 27. Parity Summary and Intentional Deviations
256	
257	Parity summary: full core context parity.
258	
259	Intentional deviations: none.
260	
261	## 28. Test Scenarios
262	
263	- context publication with all props
264	- nonce collector publication for `ArsNonceStyle`
265	- default fallback values when props are absent
266	- `use_locale()` fallback without provider
267	- `dir` attribute reactivity on locale change
268	- SSR rendering
269	- `use_style_strategy()` returns `StyleStrategy::Inline` by default
270	- `use_style_strategy()` returns configured strategy when explicitly set
271	
272	## 29. Test Oracle Notes
273	
274	| Behavior               | Preferred oracle type | Notes                                                                 |
275	| ---------------------- | --------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
276	| provider publication   | context registration  | Assert descendants observe the published ArsContext.                  |
277	| locale fallback        | context registration  | Assert `use_locale()` returns en-US without a provider.               |
278	| dir attribute          | DOM assertion         | Assert `dir` attribute matches current direction.                     |
279	| style strategy default | context registration  | Assert `use_style_strategy()` returns `Inline` without explicit prop. |
280	| nonce collector        | context registration  | Assert `append_nonce_css()` collects rules from provider context.     |
281	
282	## 30. Implementation Checklist
283	
284	- [ ] Provider context publishes all documented `ArsContext` fields.
285	- [ ] Provider publishes an `ArsNonceCssCtx` collector for `ArsNonceStyle`.
286	- [ ] `<div dir>` wrapper renders and reactively updates.
287	- [ ] Default fallback values match documented defaults (en-US, Ltr, System, false, false, Inline).
288	- [ ] `use_locale()` works with and without provider.
289	- [ ] `use_style_strategy()` returns `StyleStrategy::Inline` by default.
290	- [ ] SSR renders the wrapper with correct `dir`.
291	- [ ] Context-registration test oracles are covered.

8	
9	# ArsProvider — Dioxus Adapter
10	
11	## 1. Purpose and Adapter Scope
12	
13	This spec maps the core [`ArsProvider`](../../components/utility/ars-provider.md) context contract to Dioxus 0.7.x. `ArsProvider` is the single root provider — it subsumes the formerly separate `LocaleProvider`, `ArsStyleProvider`, and `PlatformProvider`.
14	
15	## 2. Public Adapter API
16	
17	```rust,no_check
18	#[derive(Props, Clone)]
19	pub struct ArsProviderProps {
20	    #[props(optional, into)]
21	    pub locale: Option<Signal<Locale>>,
22	    #[props(optional, into)]
23	    pub direction: Option<Signal<Direction>>,
24	    #[props(optional, into)]
25	    pub color_mode: Option<Signal<ColorMode>>,
26	    #[props(optional, into)]
27	    pub disabled: Option<Signal<bool>>,
28	    #[props(optional, into)]
29	    pub read_only: Option<Signal<bool>>,
30	    #[props(optional)]
31	    pub id_prefix: Option<String>,
32	    #[props(optional)]
33	    pub portal_container_id: Option<String>,
34	    #[props(optional)]
35	    pub root_node_id: Option<String>,
36	    #[props(optional)]
37	    pub platform: Option<Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>>,
38	    #[props(optional)]
39	    pub intl_backend: Option<Arc<dyn IntlBackend>>,
40	    #[props(optional)]
41	    pub i18n_registries: Option<Arc<I18nRegistries>>,
42	    #[props(optional)]
43	    pub style_strategy: Option<StyleStrategy>,
44	    #[props(optional)]
45	    pub dioxus_platform: Option<Arc<dyn DioxusPlatform>>,
46	    pub children: Element,
47	}
48	
49	impl PartialEq for ArsProviderProps {
50	    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool;
51	}
52	
53	#[component]
54	pub fn ArsProvider(props: ArsProviderProps) -> Element
55	```
56	
57	## 3. Mapping to Core Component Contract
58	
59	- Props parity: full parity with the core context props. Reactive props (`locale`, `direction`, `color_mode`, `disabled`, `read_only`) accept `Signal` for live updates. Non-reactive props (`id_prefix`, `portal_container_id`, `root_node_id`) are plain values set at mount time.
60	- `style_strategy` maps directly to the core `StyleStrategy` prop (defaults to `StyleStrategy::Inline`).
61	- `dioxus_platform` is an adapter-only extra prop (no core equivalent) — it provides Dioxus-specific platform services (file pickers, clipboard, drag data).
62	- `ArsProviderProps` must implement `Clone + PartialEq`, but `PartialEq` may be implemented manually so trait-object fields can use adapter-defined semantic equality.
63	- Context parity: publishes full `ArsContext` and the adapter nonce CSS collector to descendants via `use_context_provider`.
64	
65	## 4. Part Mapping
66	
67	| Core part / structure | Required?     | Adapter rendering target | Ownership      | Attr source | Notes                                 |
68	| --------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------ | -------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------- |
69	| ars provider boundary | provider-only | `<div dir="{dir}">`      | adapter-owned  | direction   | Wrapper propagates `dir` attribute.   |
70	| children subtree      | required      | consumer children        | consumer-owned | none        | Descendants consume provided context. |
71	
72	## 5. Attr Merge and Ownership Rules
73	
74	| Target node     | Core attrs | Adapter-owned attrs            | Consumer attrs | Merge order    | Ownership notes                                |
75	| --------------- | ---------- | ------------------------------ | -------------- | -------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
76	| `<div>` wrapper | none       | `dir` (derived from direction) | none           | not applicable | Minimal wrapper for BiDi direction propagation |
77	
78	## 6. Composition / Context Contract
79	
80	The adapter publishes `ArsContext` with `use_context_provider`. It also publishes an `ArsNonceCssCtx` collector owned by the provider instance and automatically renders `ArsNonceStyle` when `style_strategy` is `StyleStrategy::Nonce(nonce)`. Consumers use `try_use_context::<ArsContext>()`. The convenience hooks `use_locale()` and `use_direction()` read from this context with fallback defaults. During Dioxus SSR, all context values are platform-agnostic and require no browser globals.
81	
82	## 7. Prop Sync and Event Mapping
83	
84	ArsProvider is context-driven rather than event-driven.
85	
86	| Adapter prop                                         | Mode       | Sync trigger            | Update path                       | Visible effect                               | Notes                                                                     |
87	| ---------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ----------------------- | --------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
88	| `locale`                                             | controlled | signal change           | update ArsContext.locale          | descendants see updated locale               | direction auto-inferred when direction=None                               |
89	| `direction`                                          | controlled | signal change           | update ArsContext.direction       | `dir` attribute updates, layout reflows      | overrides locale-inferred direction                                       |
90	| `color_mode`                                         | controlled | signal change           | update ArsContext.color_mode      | theme-aware descendants re-render            |                                                                           |
91	| `disabled` / `read_only`                             | controlled | signal change           | update ArsContext fields          | descendant components enable/disable         |                                                                           |
92	| `id_prefix` / `portal_container_id` / `root_node_id` | controlled | prop change after mount | update provided context           | descendants resolve new ID prefix/targets    | non-reactive; set once at mount                                           |
93	| `style_strategy`                                     | controlled | prop set at mount       | update ArsContext.style_strategy  | descendant components use strategy for CSS   | defaults to `StyleStrategy::Inline`; `Nonce` auto-renders the nonce style |
94	| `dioxus_platform`                                    | controlled | prop set at mount       | update ArsContext.dioxus_platform | adapter-specific platform services available | defaults via feature flag resolution                                      |
95	| nonce CSS collector                                  | internal   | provider mount          | provide `ArsNonceCssCtx`          | provider-owned style renders rules           | provider-owned                                                            |
96	
97	## 8. Registration and Cleanup Contract
98	
99	- No DOM registration lifecycle beyond the `<div>` wrapper.
100	- Cleanup is the lifetime of the provided context value only.
101	- Descendants must stop reading provider data after the provider unmounts.
102	
103	| Registered entity   | Registration trigger | Identity key      | Cleanup trigger  | Cleanup action     | Notes                                             |
104	| ------------------- | -------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------- | ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------- |
105	| provided ArsContext | provider mount       | provider instance | provider cleanup | drop context value | no DOM listener ownership in the provider itself  |
106	| nonce CSS collector | provider mount       | provider instance | provider cleanup | drop context value | rendered automatically for `StyleStrategy::Nonce` |
107	
108	## 9. Ref and Node Contract
109	
110	| Target part / node | Ref required? | Ref owner      | Node availability | Composition rule | Notes                                             |
111	| ------------------ | ------------- | -------------- | ----------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
112	| `<div>` wrapper    | no            | not applicable | server-safe       | not applicable   | Renders a minimal `dir` wrapper on all platforms. |
113	
114	## 10. State Machine Boundary Rules
115	
116	- machine-owned state: provided context values exposed through `ArsContext`.
117	- adapter-local derived bookkeeping: `Memo<Direction>` derived from locale signal when direction prop is `None`.
118	- forbidden local mirrors: do not keep separate unsynchronized copies outside the published context.
119	- allowed snapshot-read contexts: provider render/effects only.
120	
121	## 11. Callback Payload Contract
122	
123	| Callback           | Payload source | Payload shape | Timing         | Cancelable? | Notes                                                      |
124	| ------------------ | -------------- | ------------- | -------------- | ----------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
125	| no public callback | none           | none          | not applicable | no          | Descendants consume context rather than callback payloads. |
126	
127	## 12. Failure and Degradation Rules
128	
129	| Condition                                                   | Policy             | Notes                                                                                  |
130	| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
131	| locale signal absent (None prop)                            | use default        | Default to `en-US`, direction inferred as `Ltr`.                                       |
132	| descendant expects required environment data that is absent | warn and ignore    | The provider remains valid; required-descendant behavior belongs to the consumer spec. |
133	| platform unavailable during SSR or server-only rendering    | degrade gracefully | All context values are platform-agnostic; no platform lookup needed.                   |
134	| `style_strategy` absent                                     | use default        | Default to `StyleStrategy::Inline`.                                                    |
135	| `dioxus_platform` absent                                    | use default        | Resolved via feature flags: Web > Desktop > NullPlatform.                              |
136	
137	## 13. Identity and Key Policy
138	
139	| Registered or repeated structure | Identity source  | Duplicates allowed? | DOM order must match registration order? | SSR/hydration stability                                        | Notes                                       |
140	| -------------------------------- | ---------------- | ------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
141	| provided ArsContext              | instance-derived | not applicable      | not applicable                           | provider instance identity must remain stable across hydration | Provider lifetime is the identity boundary. |
142	
143	## 14. SSR and Client Boundary Rules
144	
145	- The provider renders a `<div dir>` wrapper during SSR with the initial direction value.
146	- All context values are platform-agnostic (no browser dependencies in the context itself).
147	- Platform-specific operations go through `PlatformEffects` or `DioxusPlatform`, not through ArsProvider.
148	- On server rendering, no platform lookup is required — ArsProvider publishes plain configuration values.
149	
150	## 15. Performance Constraints
151	
152	- Provided context should only update when incoming signal values actually change.
153	- The `direction` memo only recomputes when locale or direction prop changes.
154	- Chain `dir` attribute off the `direction` memo to avoid redundant re-reads of `locale`.
155	
156	## 16. Implementation Dependencies
157	
158	| Dependency    | Required?   | Dependency type     | Why it must exist first                                                             | Notes                                                                                       |
159	| ------------- | ----------- | ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
160	| `dismissable` | recommended | downstream consumer | Dismissable-like utilities often consume environment context for scoped DOM access. | Dependency is about implementation order in the wider utility layer, not runtime ownership. |
161	| `focus-scope` | recommended | downstream consumer | Focus utilities may rely on scoped environment data in composite trees.             | Same guidance applies to future overlay utilities.                                          |
162	
163	## 17. Recommended Implementation Sequence
164	
165	1. Resolve all props to signals (defaulting absent signals to stored defaults).
166	2. Derive `direction` memo from locale when direction prop is `None`.
167	3. Resolve `style_strategy` (default `StyleStrategy::Inline`).
168	4. Resolve `dioxus_platform` via feature flag fallback (Web > Desktop > NullPlatform).
169	5. Build and publish provider-owned `ArsNonceCssCtx`.
170	6. Build and publish `ArsContext` via `use_context_provider`.
171	7. Render `div { dir: "{dir}", {children} }`.
172	8. Verify descendant consumption and provider cleanup.
173	
174	## 18. Anti-Patterns
175	
176	- Do not fail hard when optional props are absent — use documented defaults.
177	- Do not add heavy DOM structure beyond the `<div dir>` wrapper.
178	- Do not attempt platform lookup (e.g., `use_platform()`) inside ArsProvider — it provides configuration, not platform capabilities.
179	
180	## 19. Consumer Expectations and Guarantees
181	
182	- Consumers may assume `ArsContext` is always present when rendered inside an `ArsProvider`.
183	- Consumers may assume `use_locale()` returns a valid locale signal even without a provider (fallback to `en-US`).
184	- Consumers may assume the `dir` attribute on the wrapper reflects the current direction.
185	
186	## 20. Platform Support Matrix
187	
188	| Capability / behavior   | Web          | Desktop      | Mobile       | SSR          | Notes                                      |
189	| ----------------------- | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------------------------------------ |
190	| context publication     | full support | full support | full support | full support | All context values are platform-agnostic.  |
191	| `dir` wrapper rendering | full support | full support | full support | full support | Renders `<div dir>` on all Dioxus targets. |
192	
193	## 21. Debug Diagnostics and Production Policy
194	
195	| Condition                   | Debug build behavior | Production behavior | Notes                                    |
196	| --------------------------- | -------------------- | ------------------- | ---------------------------------------- |
197	| no ArsProvider in ancestors | debug warning        | degrade gracefully  | `use_locale()` returns fallback `en-US`. |
198	
199	## 22. Shared Adapter Helper Notes
200	
201	| Helper concept      | Required? | Responsibility                                  | Reused by                       | Notes                                   |
202	| ------------------- | --------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- |
203	| `use_locale()` hook | required  | Read locale from ArsContext with en-US fallback | all locale-dependent components | Defined in `09-adapter-dioxus.md` §16.1 |
204	
205	## 23. Framework-Specific Behavior
206	
207	Dioxus targets web, desktop, and mobile. `ArsContext` bundles platform-agnostic configuration, the `PlatformEffects` trait object, and the Dioxus-specific `DioxusPlatform` trait object. The `dioxus_platform` prop is an adapter-only extra with no core equivalent — it provides Dioxus-specific platform services (file pickers, clipboard, drag data) not covered by `PlatformEffects`. The Dioxus adapter resolves the platform implementation via feature flags: `WebPlatform` (web), `DesktopPlatform` (desktop), `NullPlatform` (SSR/tests/mobile fallback). The `style_strategy` field controls CSS injection for all descendant ars components.
208	
209	## 24. Canonical Implementation Sketch
210	
211	```rust
212	#[derive(Props, Clone)]
213	pub struct ArsProviderSketchProps {
214	    #[props(optional, into)]
215	    pub locale: Option<Signal<Locale>>,
216	    #[props(optional, into)]
217	    pub direction: Option<Signal<Direction>>,
218	    #[props(optional, into)]
219	    pub color_mode: Option<Signal<ColorMode>>,
220	    #[props(optional, into)]
221	    pub disabled: Option<Signal<bool>>,
222	    #[props(optional, into)]
223	    pub read_only: Option<Signal<bool>>,
224	    #[props(optional)]
225	    pub id_prefix: Option<String>,
226	    #[props(optional)]
227	    pub portal_container_id: Option<String>,
228	    #[props(optional)]
229	    pub root_node_id: Option<String>,
230	    #[props(optional)]
231	    pub platform: Option<Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>>,
232	    #[props(optional)]
233	    pub intl_backend: Option<Arc<dyn IntlBackend>>,
234	    #[props(optional)]
235	    pub i18n_registries: Option<Arc<I18nRegistries>>,
236	    #[props(optional)]
237	    pub style_strategy: Option<StyleStrategy>,
238	    #[props(optional)]
239	    pub dioxus_platform: Option<Arc<dyn DioxusPlatform>>,
240	    pub children: Element,
241	}
242	
243	impl PartialEq for ArsProviderSketchProps {
244	    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool;
245	}
246	
247	#[component]
248	pub fn ArsProvider(props: ArsProviderSketchProps) -> Element {
249	    let locale = props.locale.unwrap_or_else(|| {
250	        use_signal(|| Locale::parse("en-US").expect("en-US is always valid"))
251	    });
252	    let direction = use_memo(move || {
253	        props.direction
254	            .map(|d| d())
255	            .unwrap_or_else(|| locale.read().direction())
256	    });
257	    let color_mode = props.color_mode.unwrap_or_else(|| use_signal(|| ColorMode::System));
258	    let disabled = props.disabled.unwrap_or_else(|| use_signal(|| false));
259	    let read_only = props.read_only.unwrap_or_else(|| use_signal(|| false));
260	    let platform = props.platform.unwrap_or_else(|| resolve_platform_for_target());
261	    let intl_backend = props.intl_backend.unwrap_or_else(|| Arc::new(StubIntlBackend));
262	    let i18n_registries = props.i18n_registries.unwrap_or_else(|| Rc::new(I18nRegistries::new()));
263	    let style_strategy = props.style_strategy.unwrap_or(StyleStrategy::Inline);
264	    let dioxus_platform = props.dioxus_platform.unwrap_or_else(|| {
265	        #[cfg(feature = "web")]
266	        { Rc::new(WebPlatform) }
267	        #[cfg(all(feature = "desktop", not(feature = "web")))]
268	        { Rc::new(DesktopPlatform) }
269	        #[cfg(not(any(feature = "web", feature = "desktop")))]
270	        { Rc::new(NullPlatform) }
271	    });
272	
273	    let nonce_rules = use_signal(|| Vec::<NonceCssRule>::new());
274	    use_context_provider(|| ArsNonceCssCtx { rules: nonce_rules });
275	
276	    use_context_provider(|| ArsContext {
277	        locale,
278	        direction,
279	        color_mode,
280	        disabled,
281	        read_only,
282	        id_prefix: use_signal(|| props.id_prefix),
283	        portal_container_id: use_signal(|| props.portal_container_id),
284	        root_node_id: use_signal(|| props.root_node_id),
285	        platform,
286	        intl_backend,
287	        i18n_registries,
288	        style_strategy,
289	        dioxus_platform,
290	    });
291	
292	    let dir = use_memo(move || direction().as_html_attr().to_string());
293	
294	    rsx! {
295	        div { dir: "{dir}", {props.children} }
296	    }
297	}
298	```
299	
300	## 25. Adapter Invariants
301	
302	- Context publication and consumption rules must remain explicit so descendants know when environment data is available.
303	- The `dir` attribute on the wrapper MUST reactively update when direction changes.
304	- `use_locale()` MUST always return a valid signal, even without a provider.
305	- Dioxus SSR MUST NOT require a live platform context — ArsProvider publishes configuration only.
306	
307	## 26. Accessibility and SSR Notes
308	
309	The `<div dir>` wrapper propagates reading direction for correct BiDi layout. No ARIA semantics. SSR renders the wrapper with initial direction value. No platform lookup is needed during SSR.
310	
311	## 27. Parity Summary and Intentional Deviations
312	
313	Parity summary: full core context parity.
314	
315	Intentional deviations:
316	
317	- `dioxus_platform` is an adapter-only extra prop with no core equivalent. It provides Dioxus-specific platform services via the `DioxusPlatform` trait (§6 in `09-adapter-dioxus.md`).
318	
319	## 28. Test Scenarios
320	
321	- context publication with all props
322	- nonce collector publication for `ArsNonceStyle`
323	- default fallback values when props are absent
324	- `use_locale()` fallback without provider
325	- `dir` attribute reactivity on locale change
326	- SSR rendering on all Dioxus targets (web, desktop, mobile)
327	- `style_strategy` defaults to `StyleStrategy::Inline` when absent
328	- `dioxus_platform` resolves via feature flags when absent
329

331	
332	| Behavior                | Preferred oracle type | Notes                                                                                |
333	| ----------------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
334	| provider publication    | context registration  | Assert descendants observe the published ArsContext.                                 |
335	| locale fallback         | context registration  | Assert `use_locale()` returns en-US without a provider.                              |
336	| dir attribute           | DOM assertion         | Assert `dir` attribute matches current direction.                                    |
337	| SSR platform fallback   | context registration  | Verify context is published without platform lookup during SSR.                      |

341

1	//! Reactive `ArsProvider` context helpers for the Leptos adapter.
2	//!
3	//! This module wraps framework-agnostic provider values in Leptos signals and
4	//! exposes adapter-level helpers for locale, ICU, message resolution, and
5	//! reactive application text rendering.
6	
7	use std::{
8	    fmt::{self, Debug},
9	    sync::Arc,
10	};
11	
12	use ars_core::{
13	    ColorMode, DefaultModalityContext, I18nRegistries, ModalityContext, NullPlatformEffects,
14	    PlatformEffects, StyleStrategy, resolve_messages as core_resolve_messages,
15	};
16	use ars_i18n::{Direction, IntlBackend, Locale, StubIntlBackend, Translate, locales, number};
17	use leptos::{prelude::*, reactive::owner::LocalStorage};
18	
19	use crate::nonce::{ArsNonceStyle, use_nonce_css_context_provider};
20	
21	/// Reactive environment context published by the Leptos `ArsProvider`.
22	#[derive(Clone)]
23	pub struct ArsContext {
24	    /// Active locale for this subtree.
25	    pub locale: Signal<Locale>,
26	
27	    /// Resolved reading direction for this subtree.
28	    pub direction: Memo<Direction>,
29	
30	    /// Active color mode for descendants.
31	    pub color_mode: Signal<ColorMode>,
32	
33	    /// Whether interactive descendants should render as disabled.
34	    pub disabled: Signal<bool>,
35	
36	    /// Whether descendant form fields should render as read-only.
37	    pub read_only: Signal<bool>,
38	
39	    /// Optional generated-ID prefix.
40	    pub id_prefix: Signal<Option<String>>,
41	
42	    /// Optional portal container element ID.
43	    pub portal_container_id: Signal<Option<String>>,
44	
45	    /// Optional focus/portal root node ID.
46	    pub root_node_id: Signal<Option<String>>,
47	
48	    /// Platform side-effect capabilities.
49	    pub platform: Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>,
50	
51	    /// Shared input-modality state for this provider root.
52	    pub modality: Arc<dyn ModalityContext>,
53	
54	    /// ICU-backed locale data provider.
55	    pub intl_backend: Arc<dyn IntlBackend>,
56	
57	    /// Application-owned message registries.
58	    pub i18n_registries: Arc<I18nRegistries>,
59	
60	    /// CSS style injection strategy for all descendant ars components.
61	    style_strategy: StyleStrategy,
62	}
63	
64	impl Debug for ArsContext {
65	    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
66	        f.debug_struct("ArsContext")
67	            .field("locale", &self.locale)
68	            .field("direction", &self.direction)
69	            .field("color_mode", &self.color_mode)
70	            .field("disabled", &self.disabled)

168	/// Publishes adapter environment context to a Leptos subtree.
169	#[component]
170	#[expect(
171	    unreachable_pub,
172	    reason = "ArsProvider is re-exported at the adapter crate root."
173	)]
174	#[expect(
175	    clippy::too_many_arguments,
176	    reason = "ArsProvider intentionally mirrors the documented provider surface."
177	)]
178	pub fn ArsProvider<T>(
179	    #[prop(optional, into)] locale: Option<Signal<Locale>>,
180	    #[prop(optional, into)] direction: Option<Signal<Direction>>,
181	    #[prop(optional, into)] color_mode: Option<Signal<ColorMode>>,
182	    #[prop(optional, into)] disabled: Option<Signal<bool>>,
183	    #[prop(optional, into)] read_only: Option<Signal<bool>>,
184	    #[prop(optional)] id_prefix: Option<String>,
185	    #[prop(optional)] portal_container_id: Option<String>,
186	    #[prop(optional)] root_node_id: Option<String>,
187	    #[prop(optional)] platform: Option<Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>>,
188	    #[prop(optional)] intl_backend: Option<Arc<dyn IntlBackend>>,
189	    #[prop(optional)] i18n_registries: Option<Arc<I18nRegistries>>,
190	    #[prop(optional)] style_strategy: Option<StyleStrategy>,
191	    children: TypedChildren<T>,
192	) -> impl IntoView
193	where
194	    View<T>: IntoView,
195	{
196	    let locale = locale.unwrap_or_else(|| Signal::stored(locales::en_us()));
197	
198	    let direction =
199	        direction.unwrap_or_else(|| Signal::derive(move || direction_from_locale(&locale.get())));
200	
201	    let color_mode = color_mode.unwrap_or_else(|| Signal::stored(ColorMode::System));
202	
203	    let disabled = disabled.unwrap_or_else(|| Signal::stored(false));
204	
205	    let read_only = read_only.unwrap_or_else(|| Signal::stored(false));
206	
207	    let dir_attr = move || direction.get().as_html_attr();
208	
209	    let style_strategy = style_strategy.unwrap_or(StyleStrategy::Inline);
210	
211	    let nonce = match &style_strategy {
212	        StyleStrategy::Nonce(nonce) => Some(nonce.clone()),
213	        StyleStrategy::Inline | StyleStrategy::Cssom => None,
214	    };
215	
216	    #[cfg(all(feature = "hydrate", target_arch = "wasm32"))]
217	    let is_root_provider = is_root_ars_provider();
218	
219	    #[cfg(all(feature = "hydrate", target_arch = "wasm32"))]
220	    provide_ars_provider_root_marker();
221	
222	    #[cfg(all(feature = "hydrate", target_arch = "wasm32"))]
223	    Effect::new(move |_| {
224	        mark_body_hydrated_for_provider(is_root_provider);
225	    });
226	
227	    use_nonce_css_context_provider();
228	
229	    provide_ars_context(ArsContext {
230	        locale,
231	        direction: Memo::new(move |_| direction.get()),
232	        color_mode,
233	        disabled,
234	        read_only,
235	        id_prefix: Signal::stored(id_prefix),
236	        portal_container_id: Signal::stored(portal_container_id),
237	        root_node_id: Signal::stored(root_node_id),
238	        platform: platform.unwrap_or_else(|| Arc::new(NullPlatformEffects)),
239	        modality: Arc::new(DefaultModalityContext::new()),
240	        intl_backend: intl_backend.unwrap_or_else(|| Arc::new(StubIntlBackend)),
241	        i18n_registries: i18n_registries.unwrap_or_else(|| Arc::new(I18nRegistries::new())),
242	        style_strategy,
243	    });
244	
245	    view! {
246	        <div dir=dir_attr>
247	            {nonce
248	                .map(|nonce| {
249	                    let nonce = Oco::from(nonce);
250	                    view! { <ArsNonceStyle nonce /> }
251	                })} {children.into_inner()()}
252	        </div>
253	    }
254	}

122	            && self.disabled == other.disabled
123	            && self.read_only == other.read_only

142	fn arc_option_ptr_eq<T: ?Sized>(left: Option<&Arc<T>>, right: Option<&Arc<T>>) -> bool {
143	    match (left, right) {
144	        (Some(left), Some(right)) => Arc::ptr_eq(left, right),
145	        (None, None) => true,
146	        _ => false,
147	    }
148	}

150	/// Publishes adapter environment context to a Dioxus subtree.
151	#[component]
152	pub fn ArsProvider(props: ArsProviderProps) -> Element {
153	    let ArsProviderProps {
154	        locale,
155	        direction,
156	        color_mode,
157	        disabled,
158	        read_only,
159	        id_prefix,
160	        portal_container_id,
161	        root_node_id,
162	        platform,
163	        intl_backend,
164	        i18n_registries,
165	        style_strategy,
166	        dioxus_platform,
167	        children,
168	    } = props;
169	    let fallback_locale = use_signal(locales::en_us);
170	    let locale = locale.unwrap_or(fallback_locale);
171	
172	    let explicit_direction = direction;
173	
174	    let direction = use_memo(move || {
175	        explicit_direction.map_or_else(
176	            || direction_from_locale(&locale.read()),
177	            |signal| *signal.read(),
178	        )
179	    });
180	
181	    let fallback_color_mode = use_signal(|| ColorMode::System);
182	    let color_mode = color_mode.unwrap_or(fallback_color_mode);
183	
184	    let fallback_disabled = use_signal(|| false);
185	    let disabled = disabled.unwrap_or(fallback_disabled);
186	
187	    let fallback_read_only = use_signal(|| false);
188	    let read_only = read_only.unwrap_or(fallback_read_only);
189	
190	    let direction_for_context = direction;
191	
192	    let direction_for_render = direction;
193	
194	    use_nonce_css_context_provider();
195	
196	    #[cfg(all(feature = "web", target_arch = "wasm32"))]
197	    let is_root_provider = try_use_context::<ArsContext>().is_none();
198	
199	    let style_strategy = style_strategy.unwrap_or(StyleStrategy::Inline);
200	    let nonce = match &style_strategy {
201	        StyleStrategy::Nonce(nonce) => Some(nonce.clone()),
202	        StyleStrategy::Inline | StyleStrategy::Cssom => None,
203	    };
204	
205	    use_context_provider(move || ArsContext {
206	        locale,
207	        direction: direction_for_context,
208	        color_mode,
209	        disabled,
210	        read_only,
211	        id_prefix: Signal::new(id_prefix),
212	        portal_container_id: Signal::new(portal_container_id),
213	        root_node_id: Signal::new(root_node_id),
214	        platform: platform.unwrap_or_else(|| Arc::new(NullPlatformEffects)),
215	        modality: Arc::new(DefaultModalityContext::new()),
216	        intl_backend: intl_backend.unwrap_or_else(|| Arc::new(StubIntlBackend)),
217	        i18n_registries: i18n_registries.unwrap_or_else(|| Arc::new(I18nRegistries::new())),
218	        dioxus_platform: dioxus_platform.unwrap_or_else(default_dioxus_platform),
219	        style_strategy,
220	    });
221	
222	    let dir = direction_for_render.read().as_html_attr();
223	
224	    #[cfg(all(feature = "web", target_arch = "wasm32"))]
225	    use_effect(move || {
226	        if is_root_provider {
227	            crate::hydration::mark_body_hydrated();
228	        }
229	    });
230	
231	    rsx! {
232	        div { dir,
233	            if let Some(nonce) = nonce {
234	                ArsNonceStyle { nonce }
235	            }
236	            {children}
237	        }
238	    }
239	}

258

261	
262	    /// Optional portal container element ID.

265	    /// Optional focus/portal root node ID.

267	
268	    /// Platform side-effect capabilities.

276	
277	    /// Application-owned message registries.

279	
280	    /// Dioxus-specific platform services.

//! SSR tests for the Leptos `ClientOnly` adapter.

#![cfg(all(not(target_arch = "wasm32"), feature = "ssr"))]

use ars_leptos::utility::client_only::ClientOnly;
use leptos::prelude::*;

#[test]
fn client_only_renders_fallback_during_ssr() {
    let html = view! {
        <ClientOnly fallback=|| view! { <span id="client-only-fallback">"Loading"</span> }>
            <span id="client-only-child">"Client"</span>
        </ClientOnly>
    }
    .to_html();

    assert!(
        html.contains(r#"id="client-only-fallback""#),
        "fallback should render during SSR: {html}"
    );
    assert!(
        !html.contains(r#"id="client-only-child""#),
        "client children must not render during SSR: {html}"
    );
}

#[test]
fn client_only_without_fallback_renders_no_wrapper_during_ssr() {
    let html = view! {
        <section id="before"></section>
        <ClientOnly>
            <span id="client-only-child">"Client"</span>
        </ClientOnly>
        <section id="after"></section>
    }
    .to_html();

    assert!(
        html.contains(r#"id="before""#) && html.contains(r#"id="after""#),
        "siblings should still render: {html}"
    );
    assert!(
        !html.contains(r#"id="client-only-child""#),
        "client children must not render during SSR: {html}"
    );
    assert!(
        !html.contains("client-only"),
        "adapter should not invent a wrapper or marker: {html}"
    );
}
===
//! SSR tests for the Dioxus `ClientOnly` adapter.

#![cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]

use ars_dioxus::utility::client_only::ClientOnly;
use dioxus::prelude::*;

fn render_app(app: fn() -> Element) -> String {
    let mut vdom = VirtualDom::new(app);

    vdom.rebuild_in_place();

    dioxus_ssr::render(&vdom)
}

#[test]
fn client_only_renders_fallback_during_ssr() {
    fn app() -> Element {
        rsx! {
            ClientOnly {
                fallback: rsx! {
                    span { id: "client-only-fallback", "Loading" }
                },
                span { id: "client-only-child", "Client" }
            }
        }
    }

    let html = render_app(app);

    assert!(
        html.contains(r#"id="client-only-fallback""#),
        "fallback should render during SSR: {html}"
    );
    assert!(
        !html.contains(r#"id="client-only-child""#),
        "client children must not render during SSR: {html}"
    );
}

#[test]
fn client_only_without_fallback_renders_no_wrapper_during_ssr() {
    fn app() -> Element {
        rsx! {
            section { id: "before" }
            ClientOnly {
                span { id: "client-only-child", "Client" }
            }
            section { id: "after" }
        }
    }

    let html = render_app(app);

    assert!(
        html.contains(r#"id="before""#) && html.contains(r#"id="after""#),
        "siblings should still render: {html}"
    );
    assert!(
        !html.contains(r#"id="client-only-child""#),
        "client children must not render during SSR: {html}"
    );
    assert!(
        !html.contains("client-only"),
        "adapter should not invent a wrapper or marker: {html}"
    );
}
```
