```
1	//! Convenience re-exports for end users of the Leptos adapter.
2	//!
3	//! A single `use ars_leptos::prelude::*;` gives application authors access to
4	//! the components, user-facing traits, and configuration types they need —
5	//! without hunting through individual `ars_*` crates.
6	//!
7	//! # What belongs in the prelude
8	//!
9	//! Only items that **end users** interact with directly:
10	//!
11	//! 1. **Component modules** — as components land (e.g., `button`, `dialog`,
12	//!    `select`), their public module paths are re-exported here so users can
13	//!    write `button::Props`, `dialog::Machine`, etc.
14	//!
15	//! 2. **User-facing traits** — traits that end users call on component outputs
16	//!    (e.g., `Translate` from `ars-i18n` for localised strings). Re-exporting
17	//!    the trait avoids forcing users to add a direct dependency on the
18	//!    subsystem crate just to call a method.
19	//!
20	//! 3. **Configuration types** — types that appear in component props or that
21	//!    users pass to configure behaviour (e.g., `Locale`, `Direction`,
22	//!    `Orientation`, `Selection`).
23	//!
24	//! # What does NOT belong in the prelude
25	//!
26	//! - **Core engine internals** — `Machine`, `Service`, `ConnectApi`,
27	//!   `ComponentPart`, `AttrMap`, `Bindable`, `TransitionPlan`, `PendingEffect`.
28	//!   These are implementation details used by component authors *inside* the
29	//!   adapter crate, not by end users consuming components.
30	//! - **Accessibility primitives** — `AriaRole`, `AriaAttribute`, `ComponentIds`.
31	//!   Wired internally by each component; end users never construct these.
32	//! - **Interaction primitives** — `merge_attrs`, `PressState`, `FocusState`.
33	//!   Internal to component implementations.
34	//! - **Adapter hooks** — `use_machine`, `UseMachineReturn`, `EphemeralRef`.
35	//!   Used by component modules inside the adapter crate, not by end users
36	//!   who consume the ready-made components. (Still publicly accessible via
37	//!   `ars_leptos::use_machine` for advanced users building custom machines.)
38	//! - **Framework re-exports** — `leptos::prelude::*` should be imported
39	//!   separately; we do not re-export Leptos types to avoid version coupling.
40	//!
41	//! # Growth policy
42	//!
43	//! When adding a new item, ask: "Does an end user writing `<Button>` or
44	//! `<Dialog>` in their app need this?" If yes, add it. If only component
45	//! implementors inside this crate need it, keep it as a regular import.
46	
47	// -- User-facing traits --
48	pub use ars_collections::{Key, TabKey};

1	//! Convenience re-exports for end users of the Dioxus adapter.
2	//!
3	//! A single `use ars_dioxus::prelude::*;` gives application authors access to
4	//! the components, user-facing traits, and configuration types they need —
5	//! without hunting through individual `ars_*` crates.
6	//!
7	//! # What belongs in the prelude
8	//!
9	//! Only items that **end users** interact with directly:
10	//!
11	//! 1. **Component modules** — as components land (e.g., `button`, `dialog`,
12	//!    `select`), their public module paths are re-exported here so users can
13	//!    write `button::Props`, `dialog::Machine`, etc.
14	//!
15	//! 2. **User-facing traits** — traits that end users call on component outputs
16	//!    (e.g., `Translate` from `ars-i18n` for localised strings). Re-exporting
17	//!    the trait avoids forcing users to add a direct dependency on the
18	//!    subsystem crate just to call a method.
19	//!
20	//! 3. **Configuration types** — types that appear in component props or that
21	//!    users pass to configure behaviour (e.g., `Locale`, `Direction`,
22	//!    `Orientation`, `Selection`).
23	//!
24	//! # What does NOT belong in the prelude
25	//!
26	//! - **Core engine internals** — `Machine`, `Service`, `ConnectApi`,
27	//!   `ComponentPart`, `AttrMap`, `Bindable`, `TransitionPlan`, `PendingEffect`.
28	//!   These are implementation details used by component authors *inside* the
29	//!   adapter crate, not by end users consuming components.
30	//! - **Accessibility primitives** — `AriaRole`, `AriaAttribute`, `ComponentIds`.
31	//!   Wired internally by each component; end users never construct these.
32	//! - **Interaction primitives** — `merge_attrs`, `PressState`, `FocusState`.
33	//!   Internal to component implementations.
34	//! - **Adapter hooks** — `use_machine`, `UseMachineReturn`, `EphemeralRef`.
35	//!   Used by component modules inside the adapter crate, not by end users
36	//!   who consume the ready-made components. (Still publicly accessible via
37	//!   `ars_dioxus::use_machine` for advanced users building custom machines.)
38	//! - **Framework re-exports** — `dioxus::prelude::*` should be imported
39	//!   separately; we do not re-export Dioxus types to avoid version coupling.
40	//!
41	//! # Growth policy
42	//!
43	//! When adding a new item, ask: "Does an end user writing `<Button>` or
44	//! `<Dialog>` in their app need this?" If yes, add it. If only component
45	//! implementors inside this crate need it, keep it as a regular import.
46	
47	// -- User-facing traits --
48	pub use ars_collections::{Key, TabKey};
49	pub use ars_core::{I18nRegistries, MessageFn, MessagesRegistry, SafeUrl, UnsafeUrlError};
50	pub use ars_i18n::{Direction, IntlBackend, Locale, Orientation, ResolvedDirection, Translate};
51	
52	// -- Component modules --
53	//
54	// Consumers reach component types via the module qualifier
55	// (e.g. `dismissable::Props`, `dismissable::Region`, `dismissable::Handle`,
56	// `dismissable::DismissReason`, `dismissable::use_dismissable`); never via
57	// flattened aliases such as `DismissableProps` or `DismissableRegion`.
58	//
59	// The adapter `dismissable` module re-exports the agnostic
60	// `ars_components::utility::dismissable::*` surface, so this single
61	// re-export covers both the framework-agnostic types (`Props`, `Messages`,
62	// `DismissReason`, …) and the Dioxus-side wrappers (`Handle`, `Region`,
63	// `RegionProps`, `use_dismissable`).
64	pub use crate::as_child;
65	pub use crate::navigation::{self, tabs};
66	// The `error_boundary` adapter module exposes the `ArsErrorBoundary`
67	// wrapper component spec'd at
68	// `spec/foundation/09-adapter-dioxus.md` §21. End users reach it as
69	// `error_boundary::ArsErrorBoundary` after `use ars_dioxus::prelude::*;`.
70	pub use crate::utility::{
71	    self, button, client_only, dismissable, error_boundary, separator, visually_hidden,
72	    z_index_allocator,
73	};
74	// -- User-facing helpers --
75	pub use crate::{Translatable, t, use_number_formatter};
76	
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/.claude/plans/implement-tasks-347-and-giggly-crab.md",
  "content": "# Plan: Finish ArsProvider Leptos (#347) + Dioxus (#477) adapters\n\n## Context\n\nThe two adapter tasks ship the **ArsProvider** component shell — the single root context provider for every ars-ui app (locale, direction, color mode, disabled/read-only, ID prefix, portal/root nodes, platform effects, intl backend, i18n registries, style strategy, plus `dioxus_platform` on Dioxus). The agnostic core (`ArsContext`, `ColorMode`, `PlatformEffects` in `ars-core`) landed via #213; the reactive shell, hooks, and context publishing landed via #190 (Leptos) and #193 (Dioxus). #477 was then closed as superseded by #193, but the discrete *adapter component* deliverable for both frameworks was never finished:\n\n- No dedicated `tests/ars_provider*.rs` integration tests in either adapter crate.\n- `ArsProvider` is not exposed in either adapter's `prelude` — every other delivered component module is.\n- Spec §28 mandates oracles for SSR rendering, nonce auto-render when `StyleStrategy::Nonce`, default `StyleStrategy::Inline`, and (Dioxus) feature-flag-based `dioxus_platform` defaults — none of these are exercised end-to-end through the public component.\n\nThe component implementations themselves (`crates/ars-leptos/src/provider.rs` lines 169-254, `crates/ars-dioxus/src/provider.rs` lines 28-239) and ~40 inline unit/wasm tests already exist; this PR adds the missing integration-tier surface so #347 and #477 can close against a green CI + Codex 👍.\n\n## Scope\n\nOne bundled PR closing **#347** and **#477** (reopen #477 first). Add adapter-tier integration test files, expose `ArsProvider` in both preludes, fill the spec §28 gaps. No new dependencies. No spec changes (specs already authoritative). No changes to the component implementations or inline tests.\n\n## Reference: agnostic + adapter surface\n\n- Core: `crates/ars-core/src/provider.rs` (`ArsContext`, `ColorMode::{Light, Dark, System}`), `crates/ars-core/src/platform.rs` (`NullPlatformEffects`).\n- Leptos component + hooks: `crates/ars-leptos/src/provider.rs` (`ArsProvider`, `ArsContext`, `use_locale`, `use_direction`, `use_style_strategy` at `attrs.rs:1226`, `use_intl_backend`, `use_modality_context`, `use_platform_effects`, `use_number_formatter`, `t`).\n- Dioxus component + hooks: `crates/ars-dioxus/src/provider.rs` (same surface + `ArsProviderProps`, `arc_option_ptr_eq`, `default_dioxus_platform`).\n- Nonce: `crates/ars-leptos/src/nonce.rs` and `crates/ars-dioxus/src/nonce.rs` (`ArsNonceCssCtx`, `ArsNonceStyle`, `use_nonce_css_context_provider`, `NonceCssRule`, `append_nonce_css`).\n- SSR oracle pattern: `crates/ars-leptos/tests/client_only.rs` (Leptos `view!{}.to_html()`), `crates/ars-dioxus/tests/client_only.rs` (Dioxus `VirtualDom::new(app); vdom.rebuild_in_place(); dioxus_ssr::render(&vdom)`).\n- WASM probe pattern: existing inline `ProviderProbe` / `ConfiguredProviderProbe` in each adapter's `provider.rs` test module — reuse the **shape** (don't import; tests/ files can't see `pub(crate)` helpers, so define a local probe).\n\n## Files to modify\n\n### Leptos (#347)\n\n1. **Create** `crates/ars-leptos/tests/ars_provider.rs` — native SSR oracle, gated `#![cfg(all(not(target_arch = \"wasm32\"), feature = \"ssr\"))]`, mirroring the `client_only.rs` shape. Scenarios:\n   - Renders `<div dir=\"ltr\">` wrapper with children for default props (no locale supplied).\n   - Renders `<div dir=\"rtl\">` when an RTL locale (e.g. `Locale::parse(\"ar-EG\")`) is provided.\n   - Renders `<div dir=\"rtl\">` when explicit `direction=Signal::stored(Direction::Rtl)` overrides an LTR locale.\n   - When `style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Nonce(\"test-nonce\")`, the SSR HTML contains a `<style nonce=\"test-nonce\">` tag (sourced from `ArsNonceStyle`).\n   - When `style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Inline` (default), no `<style nonce=\"…\">` is emitted.\n\n2. **Create** `crates/ars-leptos/tests/ars_provider_wasm.rs` — `#![cfg(target_arch = \"wasm32\")]` + `wasm_bindgen_test_configure!(run_in_browser)`. Use a locally-defined `#[component] fn Probe()` that reads `use_context::<ArsContext>()` (reuse the pattern from the existing inline `ConfiguredProviderProbe`). Scenarios that complement (don't duplicate) the inline tests:\n   - All-props configuration round-trip: `locale`, `direction` override, `color_mode`, `disabled`, `read_only`, `id_prefix`, `portal_container_id`, `root_node_id`, `style_strategy=Cssom` — every field observable via the probe matches the input.\n   - Nonce auto-render: when `style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Nonce(...)` is passed, a probe verifies (a) `ArsNonceCssCtx` is published and (b) the rendered DOM contains a `<style>` element with the expected nonce attribute (mount via `leptos::mount::mount_to(...)` then query).\n   - `use_style_strategy()` fallback: outside any provider, returns `StyleStrategy::Inline`.\n\n3. **Edit** `crates/ars-leptos/src/prelude.rs` — add `pub use crate::ArsProvider;` and `pub use ars_core::ColorMode;` (color mode is a configuration type users pass via the `color_mode` prop; add a short doc comment on the `ColorMode` line to explain why it's in the prelude).\n\n### Dioxus (#477)\n\n4. **Create** `crates/ars-dioxus/tests/ars_provider.rs` — native SSR oracle, gated `#![cfg(not(target_arch = \"wasm32\"))]`, using the `render_app` helper from `client_only.rs`. //! Browser coverage tests for the Dioxus `Separator` adapter.

#![cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]

use std::cell::RefCell;

use ars_dioxus::{
    as_child::AsChildRenderProps,
    prelude::Orientation,
    utility::separator::{Separator, SeparatorAsChild},
};
use dioxus::prelude::*;
use wasm_bindgen_test::{wasm_bindgen_test, wasm_bindgen_test_configure};

wasm_bindgen_test_configure!(run_in_browser);

#[wasm_bindgen_test]
fn separator_browser_path_constructs_all_render_variants() {
    thread_local! {
        static RECEIVED_ATTRS: RefCell<Vec<String>> = const { RefCell::new(Vec::new()) };
    }

    RECEIVED_ATTRS.with(|attrs| attrs.borrow_mut().clear());

    fn app() -> Element {
        rsx! {
            Separator { id: "sep-wasm-horizontal" }
            Separator { id: "sep-wasm-vertical", orientation: Orientation::Vertical }
            Separator { id: "sep-wasm-decorative", decorative: true }
            Separator {}

# Plan: Finish ArsProvider Leptos (#347) + Dioxus (#477) adapters

## Context

The two adapter tasks ship the **ArsProvider** component shell — the single root context provider for every ars-ui app (locale, direction, color mode, disabled/read-only, ID prefix, portal/root nodes, platform effects, intl backend, i18n registries, style strategy, plus `dioxus_platform` on Dioxus). The agnostic core (`ArsContext`, `ColorMode`, `PlatformEffects` in `ars-core`) landed via #213; the reactive shell, hooks, and context publishing landed via #190 (Leptos) and #193 (Dioxus). #477 was then closed as superseded by #193, but the discrete *adapter component* deliverable for both frameworks was never finished:

- No dedicated `tests/ars_provider*.rs` integration tests in either adapter crate.
- `ArsProvider` is not exposed in either adapter's `prelude` — every other delivered component module is.
- Spec §28 mandates oracles for SSR rendering, nonce auto-render when `StyleStrategy::Nonce`, default `StyleStrategy::Inline`, and (Dioxus) feature-flag-based `dioxus_platform` defaults — none of these are exercised end-to-end through the public component.

The component implementations themselves (`crates/ars-leptos/src/provider.rs` lines 169-254, `crates/ars-dioxus/src/provider.rs` lines 28-239) and ~40 inline unit/wasm tests already exist; this PR adds the missing integration-tier surface so #347 and #477 can close against a green CI + Codex 👍.

## Scope

One bundled PR closing **#347** and **#477** (reopen #477 first). Add adapter-tier integration test files, expose `ArsProvider` in both preludes, fill the spec §28 gaps. No new dependencies. No spec changes (specs already authoritative). No changes to the component implementations or inline tests.

## Reference: agnostic + adapter surface

- Core: `crates/ars-core/src/provider.rs` (`ArsContext`, `ColorMode::{Light, Dark, System}`), `crates/ars-core/src/platform.rs` (`NullPlatformEffects`).
- Leptos component + hooks: `crates/ars-leptos/src/provider.rs` (`ArsProvider`, `ArsContext`, `use_locale`, `use_direction`, `use_style_strategy` at `attrs.rs:1226`, `use_intl_backend`, `use_modality_context`, `use_platform_effects`, `use_number_formatter`, `t`).
- Dioxus component + hooks: `crates/ars-dioxus/src/provider.rs` (same surface + `ArsProviderProps`, `arc_option_ptr_eq`, `default_dioxus_platform`).
- Nonce: `crates/ars-leptos/src/nonce.rs` and `crates/ars-dioxus/src/nonce.rs` (`ArsNonceCssCtx`, `ArsNonceStyle`, `use_nonce_css_context_provider`, `NonceCssRule`, `append_nonce_css`).
- SSR oracle pattern: `crates/ars-leptos/tests/client_only.rs` (Leptos `view!{}.to_html()`), `crates/ars-dioxus/tests/client_only.rs` (Dioxus `VirtualDom::new(app); vdom.rebuild_in_place(); dioxus_ssr::render(&vdom)`).
- WASM probe pattern: existing inline `ProviderProbe` / `ConfiguredProviderProbe` in each adapter's `provider.rs` test module — reuse the **shape** (don't import; tests/ files can't see `pub(crate)` helpers, so define a local probe).

## Files to modify

### Leptos (#347)

1. **Create** `crates/ars-leptos/tests/ars_provider.rs` — native SSR oracle, gated `#![cfg(all(not(target_arch = "wasm32"), feature = "ssr"))]`, mirroring the `client_only.rs` shape. Scenarios:
   - Renders `<div dir="ltr">` wrapper with children for default props (no locale supplied).
   - Renders `<div dir="rtl">` when an RTL locale (e.g. `Locale::parse("ar-EG")`) is provided.
   - Renders `<div dir="rtl">` when explicit `direction=Signal::stored(Direction::Rtl)` overrides an LTR locale.
   - When `style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Nonce("test-nonce")`, the SSR HTML contains a `<style nonce="test-nonce">` tag (sourced from `ArsNonceStyle`).
   - When `style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Inline` (default), no `<style nonce="…">` is emitted.

2. **Create** `crates/ars-leptos/tests/ars_provider_wasm.rs` — `#![cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]` + `wasm_bindgen_test_configure!(run_in_browser)`. Use a locally-defined `#[component] fn Probe()` that reads `use_context::<ArsContext>()` (reuse the pattern from the existing inline `ConfiguredProviderProbe`). Scenarios that complement (don't duplicate) the inline tests:
   - All-props configuration round-trip: `locale`, `direction` override, `color_mode`, `disabled`, `read_only`, `id_prefix`, `portal_container_id`, `root_node_id`, `style_strategy=Cssom` — every field observable via the probe matches the input.
   - Nonce auto-render: when `style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Nonce(...)` is passed, a probe verifies (a) `ArsNonceCssCtx` is published and (b) the rendered DOM contains a `<style>` element with the expected nonce attribute (mount via `leptos::mount::mount_to(...)` then query).
   - `use_style_strategy()` fallback: outside any provider, returns `StyleStrategy::Inline`.

3. **Edit** `crates/ars-leptos/src/prelude.rs` — add `pub use crate::ArsProvider;` and `pub use ars_core::ColorMode;` (color mode is a configuration type users pass via the `color_mode` prop; add a short doc comment on the `ColorMode` line to explain why it's in the prelude).

### Dioxus (#477)

4. **Create** `crates/ars-dioxus/tests/ars_provider.rs` — native SSR oracle, gated `#![cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]`, using the `render_app` helper from `client_only.rs`. Scenarios:
   - Default props render `<div dir="ltr">` with children.
   - RTL locale renders `<div dir="rtl">`.
   - Explicit `direction` prop overrides locale-derived direction.
   - `style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Nonce("test-nonce")` emits `<style nonce="test-nonce">`.
   - Default (`None`) `style_strategy` emits no nonce style.
   - **Dioxus-specific:** when `dioxus_platform` is `None`, the published context's `dioxus_platform` resolves to `default_dioxus_platform()` (verified via a probe component reading `use_context::<ArsContext>()`, since this is a context-registration oracle per spec §29).

5. **Create** `crates/ars-dioxus/tests/ars_provider_wasm.rs` — `#![cfg(all(target_arch = "wasm32", feature = "web"))]` + `wasm_bindgen_test_configure!(run_in_browser)`. Scenarios analogous to Leptos wasm tests, plus:
   - `dioxus_platform` resolution when no prop supplied (context-registration oracle).
   - Nonce DOM presence verified via mount + querySelector.

6. **Edit** `crates/ars-dioxus/src/prelude.rs` — add `pub use crate::{ArsProvider, ArsProviderProps};` and `pub use ars_core::ColorMode;` symmetric to Leptos. (Dioxus needs the `Props` struct re-exported because explicit Props derives are part of the user-callable surface.)

### Both adapters

7. Run `post-implementation-audit` skill on the diff: spec drift pass (compare new tests against §28/§29 oracles + §30 checklist), iterative "anything missing?" pass (≥2 rounds), test-coverage audit (`cargo llvm-cov test -p ars-leptos --text -- ars_provider` and same for Dioxus; uncovered branches must be intentional). Land every audit finding in this PR — no follow-ups.

8. Reopen #477 (`gh issue reopen 477`) so the PR's `Closes #477` auto-close keyword works.

9. Open one PR targeting `main` with body containing `Closes #347` and `Closes #477`. Wait for codex review via the `waiting-for-codex-review` skill; address every thread.

## Spec parity notes (not changing the spec)

- Issue #347's "Tests to add first" template lists "ARIA attributes from core ConnectApi" and "Axe-core audit using `data-ars-scope` selector". Neither applies to `ArsProvider`: spec §1.2 documents it as a context-only provider with no ConnectApi/`Part`/`data-ars-scope` (the `<div dir>` wrapper has zero ARIA semantics by design — spec §26). The PR description should call this out explicitly so the reviewer doesn't expect those tests. Spec §28 is the authoritative test-scenarios list and is what we're delivering against.
- Leptos task description references "ZIndexAllocator and ClientOnly Leptos adapter" (#319) as a dependency — already closed and shipped. No action needed.

## Verification

Run from the worktree root (`/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-272d6151`):

1. **Targeted unit/integration:**
   - `cargo test -p ars-leptos --features ssr --test ars_provider`
   - `cargo test -p ars-dioxus --test ars_provider`
   - Wasm: `wasm-pack test --chrome --headless crates/ars-leptos --test ars_provider_wasm` (or use the workspace recipe used by existing `*_wasm` files; check `Cargo.toml` for the canonical command).
2. **Coverage probe (per crate, per CLAUDE.md):**
   - `cargo llvm-cov test -p ars-leptos --text -- ars_provider` — inspect uncovered branches in `provider.rs` lines around the `ArsProvider` component body; verify nonce + style strategy branches are now hit.
   - Same for `ars-dioxus`.
3. **Workspace gate before push:** `cargo xci-fast` (≈5 min, includes fmt, check, clippy, unit/integration/adapter, spec-compile-snippets, error-variant-coverage, snapshot-count).
4. **Codex review:** invoke `waiting-for-codex-review` skill after first push; loop until 👍.
5. **Manual eyeball on PR:** confirm `Closes #347` and `Closes #477` keywords are present; #477 reopened before PR opens; both preludes symmetric.

## Out of scope (explicitly)

- No changes to `ArsProvider` component implementation in either `provider.rs` — already spec-compliant.
- No refactoring of existing inline tests — they cover unit-level concerns and stay where they are.
- No new dependencies — no `Cargo.toml` edits.
- No spec edits — spec is authoritative and unchanged.
- `ArsContext` does not enter the prelude (it's a published context value, not a configuration type users pass in). Advanced users still reach it via `ars_leptos::ArsContext` / `ars_dioxus::ArsContext`.

38	# Pre-compiled CLDR data via ICU4X. Default backend on native targets and the
39	# server-side rendering path. Use `--no-default-features --features web,ars-i18n/web-intl`
40	# to swap icu4x for the browser-native Intl API on wasm32 builds. Also enables
41	# `ars-components/i18n` so locale-aware components (e.g., `Highlight`) are
42	# available in the adapter build.
43	icu4x = ["ars-i18n/std", "ars-i18n/icu4x", "ars-components/i18n"]

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-272d6151/crates/ars-leptos/tests/ars_provider.rs",
  "content": "//! SSR oracle tests for the Leptos `ArsProvider` adapter.\n//!\n//! These tests cover spec §28 scenarios from\n//! `spec/leptos-components/utility/ars-provider.md`:\n//! `<div dir>` wrapper rendering, direction inference vs. explicit override,\n//! and provider-owned auto-render of `ArsNonceStyle` when\n//! `style_strategy` is `StyleStrategy::Nonce(...)`. The wasm DOM and\n//! reactive-update oracles live in `tests/ars_provider_wasm.rs` and the\n//! inline `wasm_tests` module in `src/provider.rs`.\n\n#![cfg(all(not(target_arch = \"wasm32\"), feature = \"ssr\"))]\n\nuse ars_core::{ColorMode, StyleStrategy};\nuse ars_i18n::{Direction, Locale};\nuse ars_leptos::ArsProvider;\nuse leptos::prelude::*;\n\nfn render_with(view: impl IntoView) -> String {\n    view.into_view().to_html()\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_renders_ltr_wrapper_for_default_props() {\n    let html = render_with(view! {\n        <ArsProvider>\n            <span id=\"child\">\"hello\"</span>\n        </ArsProvider>\n    });\n\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"dir=\"ltr\"\"#),\n        \"default provider should render dir=\\\"ltr\\\": {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"id=\"child\"\"#),\n        \"children should render inside the provider wrapper: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(\"<style nonce\"),\n        \"default StyleStrategy::Inline must not emit a nonce style tag: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_infers_rtl_direction_from_rtl_locale() {\n    let locale = Signal::stored(Locale::parse(\"ar-SA\").expect(\"locale should parse\"));\n\n    let html = render_with(view! {\n        <ArsProvider locale=locale>\n            <span id=\"child\">\"hello\"</span>\n        </ArsProvider>\n    });\n\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"dir=\"rtl\"\"#),\n        \"RTL locale should produce dir=\\\"rtl\\\": {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"id=\"child\"\"#),\n        \"children should render inside the RTL wrapper: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_explicit_direction_overrides_locale_inferred_direction() {\n    let locale = Signal::stored(Locale::parse(\"ar-SA\").expect(\"locale should parse\"));\n    let direction = Signal::stored(Direction::Ltr);\n\n    let html = render_with(view! {\n        <ArsProvider locale=locale direction=direction>\n            <span id=\"child\">\"hello\"</span>\n        </ArsProvider>\n    });\n\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"dir=\"ltr\"\"#),\n        \"explicit direction prop must override locale-inferred RTL: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(r#\"dir=\"rtl\"\"#),\n        \"no rtl marker should remain after explicit override: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_with_nonce_style_strategy_emits_style_tag_with_nonce_attribute() {\n    let html = render_with(view! {\n        <ArsProvider style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Nonce(String::from(\"test-nonce\"))>\n            <span id=\"child\">\"hello\"</span>\n        </ArsProvider>\n    });\n\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"<style nonce=\"test-nonce\"\"#),\n        \"StyleStrategy::Nonce must auto-render <style nonce=...> tag: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"id=\"child\"\"#),\n        \"children must still render alongside the nonce style tag: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_with_inline_style_strategy_emits_no_nonce_style_tag() {\n    let html = render_with(view! {\n        <ArsProvider style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Inline>\n            <span id=\"child\">\"hello\"</span>\n        </ArsProvider>\n    });\n\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(\"<style nonce\"),\n        \"StyleStrategy::Inline must not emit a nonce style tag: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_with_cssom_style_strategy_emits_no_nonce_style_tag() {\n    let html = render_with(view! {\n        <ArsProvider style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Cssom>\n            <span id=\"child\">\"hello\"</span>\n        </ArsProvider>\n    });\n\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(\"<style nonce\"),\n        \"StyleStrategy::Cssom must not emit a nonce style tag: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_renders_color_mode_disabled_and_read_only_without_extra_attributes_on_wrapper() {\n    let html = render_with(view! {\n        <ArsProvider\n            color_mode=Signal::stored(ColorMode::Dark)\n            disabled=Signal::stored(true)\n            read_only=Signal::stored(true)\n        >\n            <span id=\"child\">\"hello\"</span>\n        </ArsProvider>\n    });\n\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"dir=\"ltr\"\"#),\n        \"wrapper still emits dir attribute regardless of color/disabled/read-only props: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(r#\"data-color-mode\"#),\n        \"provider wrapper must not emit color-mode markup (consumer-owned): {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(r#\"aria-disabled\"#),\n        \"provider wrapper must not emit aria-disabled (descendants own that semantics): {html}\"\n    );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "//! Browser tests for the Leptos `ArsProvider` adapter.\n//!\n//! Complements the SSR oracle (`tests/ars_provider.rs`) and the inline wasm\n//! tests in `src/provider.rs::wasm_tests`. These tests cover the spec §28\n//! scenarios that require a real DOM:\n//!\n//! - All-props configuration round-trip observable via context.\n//! - Nonce auto-render attaches a `<style nonce=\"...\">` element under the\n//!   provider boundary.\n//! - `use_style_strategy()` falls back to `StyleStrategy::Inline` outside of\n//!   any provider.\n\n#![cfg(target_arch = \"wasm32\")]\n\nuse ars_core::{ColorMode, StyleStrategy};\nuse ars_i18n::{Direction, Locale};\nuse ars_leptos::{\n    ArsContext, ArsNonceCssCtx, ArsProvider, append_nonce_css, use_style_strategy,\n};\nuse leptos::{mount::mount_to, prelude::*};\nuse wasm_bindgen::JsCast;\nuse wasm_bindgen_test::{wasm_bindgen_test, wasm_bindgen_test_configure};\n\nwasm_bindgen_test_configure!(run_in_browser);\n\nfn document() -> web_sys::Document {\n    web_sys::window()\n        .and_then(|window| window.document())\n        .expect(\"document should exist\")\n}\n\nfn container() -> web_sys::HtmlElement {\n    let element = document()\n        .create_element(\"div\")\n        .expect(\"container should be created\");\n\n    document()\n        .body()\n        .expect(\"body should exist\")\n        .append_child(&element)\n        .expect(\"container should attach\");\n\n    element\n        .dyn_into::<web_sys::HtmlElement>()\n        .expect(\"container should be an HtmlElement\")\n}\n\n#[leptos::component]\nfn ConfigProbe() -> impl IntoView {\n    let context = use_context::<ArsContext>().expect(\"ArsProvider should publish ArsContext\");\n    let strategy = use_style_strategy();\n\n    let direction = context.direction;\n    let locale = context.locale;\n    let color_mode = context.color_mode;\n    let disabled = context.disabled;\n    let read_only = context.read_only;\n    let id_prefix = context.id_prefix;\n    let portal_container_id = context.portal_container_id;\n    let root_node_id = context.root_node_id;\n\n    view! {\n        <div data-testid=\"config-probe\">\n            <span data-testid=\"locale\">{move || locale.get().to_bcp47()}</span>\n            <span data-testid=\"direction\">\n                {move || direction.get().as_html_attr()}\n            </span>\n            <span data-testid=\"color-mode\">\n                {move || format!(\"{:?}\", color_mode.get())}\n            </span>\n            <span data-testid=\"disabled\">{move || disabled.get().to_string()}</span>\n            <span data-testid=\"read-only\">{move || read_only.get().to_string()}</span>\n            <span data-testid=\"id-prefix\">\n                {move || id_prefix.get().unwrap_or_default()}\n            </span>\n            <span data-testid=\"portal-container-id\">\n                {move || portal_container_id.get().unwrap_or_default()}\n            </span>\n            <span data-testid=\"root-node-id\">\n                {move || root_node_id.get().unwrap_or_default()}\n            </span>\n            <span data-testid=\"style-strategy\">\n                {move || format!(\"{:?}\", strategy.get())}\n            </span>\n        </div>\n    }\n}\n\n#[leptos::component]\nfn NonceProbe() -> impl IntoView {\n    let nonce_context =\n        use_context::<ArsNonceCssCtx>().expect(\"ArsProvider should publish nonce context\");\n\n    append_nonce_css(String::from(\".probe { color: red; }\"));\n\n    view! {\n        <span\n            data-testid=\"nonce-rules-count\"\n        >\n            {move || nonce_context.rules.get().len().to_string()}\n        </span>\n    }\n}\n\n#[leptos::component]\nfn FallbackProbe() -> impl IntoView {\n    let strategy = use_style_strategy();\n\n    view! {\n        <span data-testid=\"fallback-style-strategy\">\n            {move || format!(\"{:?}\", strategy.get())}\n        </span>\n    }\n}\n\nfn text_for(container: &web_sys::HtmlElement, selector: &str) -> String {\n    container\n        .query_selector(selector)\n        .expect(\"selector should be valid\")\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"{selector} should exist\"))\n        .text_content()\n        .expect(\"element should have text content\")\n}\n\n#[cfg(feature = \"csr\")]\n#[wasm_bindgen_test]\nasync fn ars_provider_publishes_all_configured_fields_to_descendants_on_wasm() {\n    let container = container();\n\n    let mount_handle = mount_to(container.clone(), move || {\n        view! {\n            <ArsProvider\n                locale=Signal::stored(Locale::parse(\"es-ES\").expect(\"locale should parse\"))\n                direction=Signal::stored(Direction::Rtl)\n                color_mode=Signal::stored(ColorMode::Dark)\n                disabled=Signal::stored(true)\n                read_only=Signal::stored(true)\n                id_prefix=String::from(\"app-prefix\")\n                portal_container_id=String::from(\"portal-root\")\n                root_node_id=String::from(\"focus-root\")\n                style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Cssom\n            >\n                <ConfigProbe />\n            </ArsProvider>\n        }\n    });\n\n    leptos::task::tick().await;\n\n    assert_eq!(text_for(&container, \"[data-testid='locale']\"), \"es-ES\");\n    assert_eq!(text_for(&container, \"[data-testid='direction']\"), \"rtl\");\n    assert_eq!(text_for(&container, \"[data-testid='color-mode']\"), \"Dark\");\n    assert_eq!(text_for(&container, \"[data-testid='disabled']\"), \"true\");\n    assert_eq!(text_for(&container, \"[data-testid='read-only']\"), \"true\");\n    assert_eq!(text_for(&container, \"[data-testid='id-prefix']\"), \"app-prefix\");\n    assert_eq!(\n        text_for(&container, \"[data-testid='portal-container-id']\"),\n        \"portal-root\"\n    );\n    assert_eq!(\n        text_for(&container, container\n        .query_selector(\"[dir='rtl']\")\n        .expect(\"selector should be valid\")\n        .expect(\"RTL wrapper should exist\");\n\n    assert_eq!(wrapper.get_attribute(\"dir\").as_deref(), Some(\"rtl\"));\n\n    drop(mount_handle);\n\n    container.remove();\n}\n\n#[cfg(feature = \"csr\")]\n#[wasm_bindgen_test]\nasync fn ars_provider_with_nonce_strategy_auto_renders_style_tag_in_dom_on_wasm() {\n    let container = container();\n\n    let mount_handle = mount_to(container.clone(), move || {\n        view! {\n            <ArsProvider style_strategy=StyleStrategy::Nonce(String::from(\"dom-nonce\"))>\n                <NonceProbe />\n            </ArsProvider>\n        }\n    });\n\n    leptos::task::tick().await;\n\n .text_content()\n        .expect(\"style tag should have CSS text\");\n\n    assert!(\n        css.contains(\".probe { color: red; }\"),\n        \"appended CSS rules should land in the nonce style tag: {css}\"\n    );\n\n    drop(mount_handle);\n\n    container.remove();\n}\n\n#[cfg(feature = \"csr\")]\n#[wasm_bindgen_test]\nasync fn use_style_strategy_falls_back_to_inline_without_provider_on_wasm() {\n    let container = container();\n\n    let mount_handle = mount_to(container.clone(), move || {\n        view! { <FallbackProbe /> }\n    });\n\n    leptos::task::tick().await;\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        text_for(&container, \"[data-testid='fallback-style-strategy']\"),\n        \"Inline\",\n        \"use_style_strategy must default to Inline without ArsProvider in scope\"\n    );\n\n    drop(mount_handle);\n\n    container.remove();\n}\n"

1	//! Dioxus-specific platform abstraction (`DioxusPlatform`) and its
2	//! per-target implementations.
3	//!
4	//! This module covers operations the framework-agnostic
5	//! [`ars_core::PlatformEffects`] trait does **not** expose: file pickers,
6	//! clipboard writes, drag-data extraction, bounding rects, UUID generation,
7	//! and high-precision timestamps. It is the Dioxus-side analog to the
8	//! browser-only surface that `ars-leptos` would otherwise inline.
9	//!
10	//! The trait flows into adapter components via
11	//! [`ArsContext::dioxus_platform`](crate::ArsContext#structfield.dioxus_platform);
12	//! components retrieve the active implementation through [`use_platform`].
13	//!
14	//! See `spec/foundation/09-adapter-dioxus.md` §6 for the contract.
15	
16	use std::{pin::Pin, rc::Rc, sync::Arc, time::Duration};
17	#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
18	use std::{sync::LazyLock, time::Instant};
19	
20	use ars_core::Rect;
21	use ars_forms::field::FileRef;
22	use ars_interactions::{DragItem, FileHandle};
23	use dioxus::{
24	    events::{DragData as DioxusDragData, MountedData, ScrollBehavior},
25	    prelude::try_use_context,
26	};
27	
28	use crate::{ArsContext, warn_missing_provider};
29	
30	/// Options for opening a platform file picker.
31	///
32	/// Mirrors the subset of HTML `<input type="file">` configuration that the
33	/// adapter exposes. Web targets ignore these options because the real file
34	/// picker is hosted by the `FileUpload` component's hidden `<input>`; desktop
35	/// targets translate them into `rfd` native-dialog filters.
36	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
37	pub struct FilePickerOptions {
38	    /// Accepted MIME types or extensions. Empty means accept any.
39	    pub accept: Vec<String>,
40	
41	    /// Whether the user may select more than one file at once.
42	    pub multiple: bool,
43	}
44	
45	/// Adapter-local drag payload extracted from a Dioxus drag event.
46	///
47	/// Mirrors `spec/components/utility/drop-zone.md` §1.3. The `items`
48	/// vector contains the structured drag payload (text, URI, files,
49	/// etc.) when the platform exposes it; `types` carries MIME types
50	/// advertised by the drag source for the formats this adapter is able
51	/// to detect.
52	///
53	/// **What's extracted, by target:**
54	///
55	/// - **Web (wasm32 + `feature = "web"`)**: items as `DragItem::File`
56	///   for each `DataTransfer.files()` entry; `types` populated from the
57	///   subset of default probe formats for which `data_transfer.get_data()`
58	///   returns a non-empty value.
59	/// - **Desktop (`feature = "desktop"`)**: items as `DragItem::File`
60	///   for each path in wry's `DragDropEvent::Drop { paths, .. }`. Each
61	///   item carries a real `FileHandle::from_path(_)` so the drop-zone
62	///   adapter can resolve content. `types` is best-effort via the same
63	///   non-empty `get_data()` probe.
64	/// - **`PlatformDragEvent::empty()`** (any target): both fields empty.
65	///
66	/// `dioxus_html::DataTransfer` does not expose a `types()` accessor on
67	/// the public API (only `get_data(format)`), so the type list is built
68	/// by probing a fixed set of common formats rather than enumerating
69	/// the source's full advertised list. Components that need a specific
70	/// format outside the default probes should call
71	/// `event.data_transfer().get_data(format)` directly.
72	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
73	pub struct DragData {
74	    /// The dragged items.
75	    ///
76	    /// May be empty on `DragEnter` / `DragOver` when the browser
77	    /// restricts access to item content until drop (security
78	    /// restriction).
79	    pub items: Vec<DragItem>,
80

670	/// "clipboard writes succeed but do nothing" semantics into a misconfigured
671	/// production build. The `mobile` feature also currently lands at step 3.
672	///
673	/// `WebPlatform` and `DesktopPlatform` are rendered as plain code rather
674	/// than intra-doc links because their types are cfg-gated out of scope on
675	/// the docs build target.
676	///
677	/// This function exists alongside [`use_platform`] for callers that need a
678	/// platform handle outside a Dioxus render scope (test harnesses, SSR
679	/// bootstrap, benches). Inside components, prefer [`use_platform`].
680	#[must_use]
681	pub fn default_dioxus_platform() -> Arc<dyn DioxusPlatform> {
682	    #[cfg(all(feature = "web", target_arch = "wasm32"))]
683	    {
684	        Arc::new(WebPlatform)
685	    }
686	
687	    #[cfg(all(feature = "desktop", not(all(feature = "web", target_arch = "wasm32"))))]
688	    {
689	        Arc::new(DesktopPlatform)
690	    }
691	
692	    #[cfg(all(
693	        not(all(feature = "web", target_arch = "wasm32")),
694	        not(feature = "desktop")
695	    ))]
696	    {
697	        Arc::new(NullPlatform)
698	    }
699	}
700	
701	/// Resolves the active [`DioxusPlatform`] from the surrounding
702	/// [`ArsProvider`](crate::ArsProvider) context.
703	///
704	/// When no [`ArsProvider`](crate::ArsProvider) is mounted, falls back to
705	/// [`default_dioxus_platform`] using the resolution order
706	/// **`web` → `desktop` → [`NullPlatform`]**. The `mobile` feature currently
707	/// falls through to [`NullPlatform`]; when a dedicated mobile platform is
708	/// added, update [`default_dioxus_platform`] with a
709	/// `#[cfg(feature = "mobile")]` arm.
710	#[must_use]
711	pub fn use_platform() -> Arc<dyn DioxusPlatform> {
712	    try_use_context::<ArsContext>().map_or_else(
713	        || {
714	            warn_missing_provider("use_platform");
715	
716	            default_dioxus_platform()
717	        },
718	        |ctx| Arc::clone(&ctx.dioxus_platform),
719	    )
720	}
721

374	/// Web platform implementation backed by `web_sys`.
375	///
376	/// Only exists on `wasm32` targets even when the `web` feature is enabled —
377	/// every method invokes browser APIs that have no meaningful native
378	/// fallback. On non-wasm hosts (e.g., `cargo check --features web` on a
379	/// developer's Linux box, or a misconfigured production build), the type
380	/// is *not in scope* and [`default_dioxus_platform`] falls through to
381	/// [`NullPlatform`] instead. This keeps build tooling green without
382	/// silently degrading runtime behaviour into "clipboard writes succeed but
383	/// do nothing" surprises.
384	#[cfg(all(feature = "web", target_arch = "wasm32"))]
385	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
386	pub struct WebPlatform;
387	
388	#[cfg(all(feature = "web", target_arch = "wasm32"))]
389	impl DioxusPlatform for WebPlatform {
390	    fn set_clipboard(&self, text: &str) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<(), String>>>> {
391	        let text = text.to_string();
392	
393	        Box::pin(async move {
394	            let window = web_sys::window().ok_or_else(|| String::from("no window available"))?;
395	
396	            let promise = window.navigator().clipboard().write_text(&text);
397	
398	            wasm_bindgen_futures::JsFuture::from(promise)
399	                .await
400	                .map(|_| ())
401	                .map_err(|err| format!("{err:?}"))
402	        })
403	    }
404

  "content": "//! SSR oracle tests for the Dioxus `ArsProvider` adapter.\n//!\n//! Covers spec §28 / §29 scenarios from\n//! `spec/dioxus-components/utility/ars-provider.md`: `<div dir>` wrapper\n//! rendering, direction inference vs. explicit override, provider-owned\n//! auto-render of `ArsNonceStyle` when `style_strategy` is\n//! `StyleStrategy::Nonce(...)`, and `dioxus_platform` default resolution via\n//! feature flags. Browser DOM and reactive-update oracles live in the inline\n//! `wasm_tests` module of `src/provider.rs` and in `tests/ars_provider_wasm.rs`.\n\n#![cfg(not(target_arch = \"wasm32\"))]\n\nuse ars_core::{ColorMode, StyleStrategy};\nuse ars_dioxus::{ArsContext, ArsProvider, ArsProviderProps};\nuse ars_i18n::{Direction, Locale};\nuse dioxus::prelude::*;\n\nfn render_app(app: fn() -> Element) -> String {\n    let mut vdom = VirtualDom::new(app);\n\n    vdom.rebuild_in_place();\n\n    dioxus_ssr::render(&vdom)\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_renders_ltr_wrapper_for_default_props() {\n    fn app() -> Element {\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider {\n                span { id: \"child\", \"hello\" }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let html = render_app(app);\n\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"dir=\"ltr\"\"#),\n        \"default provider should render dir=\\\"ltr\\\": {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"id=\"child\"\"#),\n        \"children should render inside the provider wrapper: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(\"<style nonce\"),\n        \"default StyleStrategy::Inline must not emit a nonce style tag: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_infers_rtl_direction_from_rtl_locale() {\n    fn app() -> Element {\n        let locale = use_signal(|| Locale::parse(\"ar-SA\").expect(\"locale should parse\"));\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider { locale,\n                span { id: \"child\", \"hello\" }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let html = render_app(app);\n\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"dir=\"rtl\"\"#),\n        \"RTL locale should produce dir=\\\"rtl\\\": {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"id=\"child\"\"#),\n        \"children should render inside the RTL wrapper: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_explicit_direction_overrides_locale_inferred_direction() {\n    fn app() -> Element {\n        let locale = use_signal(|| Locale::parse(\"ar-SA\").expect(\"locale should parse\"));\n        let direction = use_signal(|| Direction::Ltr);\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider { locale, direction,\n                span { id: \"child\", \"hello\" }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let html = render_app(app);\n\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"dir=\"ltr\"\"#),\n        \"explicit direction prop must override locale-inferred RTL: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(r#\"dir=\"rtl\"\"#),\n        \"no rtl marker should remain after explicit override: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_with_nonce_style_strategy_emits_style_tag_with_nonce_attribute() {\n    fn app() -> Element {\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider {\n                style_strategy: StyleStrategy::Nonce(String::from(\"test-nonce\")),\n                span { id: \"child\", \"hello\" }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let html = render_app(app);\n\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"<style nonce=\"test-nonce\"\"#),\n        \"StyleStrategy::Nonce must auto-render <style nonce=...> tag: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"id=\"child\"\"#),\n        \"children must still render alongside the nonce style tag: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_with_inline_style_strategy_emits_no_nonce_style_tag() {\n    fn app() -> Element {\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider {\n                style_strategy: StyleStrategy::Inline,\n                span { id: \"child\", \"hello\" }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let html = render_app(app);\n\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(\"<style nonce\"),\n        \"StyleStrategy::Inline must not emit a nonce style tag: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_with_cssom_style_strategy_emits_no_nonce_style_tag() {\n    fn app() -> Element {\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider {\n                style_strategy: StyleStrategy::Cssom,\n                span { id: \"child\", \"hello\" }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let html = render_app(app);\n\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(\"<style nonce\"),\n        \"StyleStrategy::Cssom must not emit a nonce style tag: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[component]\nfn PlatformProbe() -> Element {\n    let context =\n        try_use_context::<ArsContext>().expect(\"ArsProvider should publish ArsContext\");\n\n    let id = context.dioxus_platform.new_id();\n\n    rsx! {\n        span { \"data-testid\": \"platform-id\", \"{id}\" }\n    }\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_resolves_dioxus_platform_default_via_feature_flag_when_prop_absent() {\n    fn app() -> Element {\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider { PlatformProbe {} }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let html = render_app(app);\n\n    // SSR runs on the native target without `web` or `desktop` features, so\n    // `default_dioxus_platform()` resolves to `NullPlatform`, whose `new_id()`\n    // produces strings prefixed with `null-id-`. This pins the §29 oracle that\n    // `use_platform()` returns the feature-flag-appropriate impl.\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"data-testid=\"platform-id\"\"#),\n        \"PlatformProbe should render inside the provider: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(\"null-id-\"),\n        \"default DioxusPlatform on native (no web/desktop) is NullPlatform; \\\n         its new_id() output should appear in the rendered HTML: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_renders_color_mode_disabled_and_read_only_without_extra_wrapper_attrs() {\n    fn app() -> Element {\n        let color_mode = use_signal(|| ColorMode::Dark);\n        let disabled = use_signal(|| true);\n        let read_only = use_signal(|| true);\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider { color_mode, disabled, read_only,\n                span { id: \"child\", \"hello\" }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let html = render_app(app);\n\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"dir=\"ltr\"\"#),\n        \"wrapper still emits dir attribute regardless of color/disabled/read-only props: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(r#\"data-color-mode\"#),\n        \"provider wrapper must not emit color-mode markup: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        !html.contains(r#\"aria-disabled\"#),\n        \"provider wrapper must not emit aria-disabled: {html}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_props_default_is_eq_to_itself() {\n    let lhs = ArsProviderProps {\n        locale: None,\n        direction: None,\n        color_mode: None,\n        disabled: None,\n        read_only: None,\n        id_prefix: None,\n        portal_container_id: None,\n        root_node_id: None,\n        platform: None,\n        intl_backend: None,\n        i18n_registries: None,\n        style_strategy: None,\n        dioxus_platform: None,\n        children: VNode::placeholder(),\n    };\n    let rhs = lhs.clone();\n\n    assert_eq!(lhs, rhs, \"ArsProviderProps PartialEq must be reflexive\");\n}\n"
}

1	//! Browser tests for the Dioxus Tabs adapter.
2	//!
3	//! Verifies the wasm32 build path: that the adapter and its wasm-only
4	//! helpers (`MountedData` focus, reactive prop sync, lazy-mount
5	//! tracking) compile, that the `VirtualDom` lifecycle drives the
6	//! reactive contract, and that the rendered structure is observable
7	//! through the connect API.
8	//!
9	//! Most tests use `VirtualDom`; focused DOM-event coverage uses
10	//! `dioxus-web` launch for browser-only behavior such as canceled link
11	//! activation.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-272d6151/crates/ars-dioxus/tests/ars_provider_wasm.rs",
  "content": "//! Browser tests for the Dioxus `ArsProvider` adapter.\n//!\n//! Complements the SSR oracle (`tests/ars_provider.rs`) and the inline\n//! `wasm_tests` module of `src/provider.rs`. The inline tests cover\n//! mutation-log oracles for context publication and reactivity; this file\n//! adds the integration-tier scenarios that require a real DOM mount via\n//! `dioxus_web::launch::launch_virtual_dom`:\n//!\n//! - Provider auto-renders `<style nonce=\"...\">` into the DOM when\n//!   `style_strategy` is `StyleStrategy::Nonce(...)`.\n//! - `use_platform()` outside any `ArsProvider` falls back to\n//!   `default_dioxus_platform()`, which on `wasm32 + feature = \"web\"` is\n//!   `WebPlatform`. The fingerprint is `new_id()` returning a UUID rather\n//!   than `NullPlatform`'s `null-id-N` strings.\n\n#![cfg(all(target_arch = \"wasm32\", feature = \"web\"))]\n\nuse std::{cell::RefCell, rc::Rc};\n\nuse ars_core::StyleStrategy;\nuse ars_dioxus::{ArsContext, ArsProvider, default_dioxus_platform, use_platform};\nuse dioxus::prelude::*;\nuse wasm_bindgen::JsCast;\nuse wasm_bindgen_test::{wasm_bindgen_test, wasm_bindgen_test_configure};\n\nwasm_bindgen_test_configure!(run_in_browser);\n\nfn document() -> web_sys::Document {\n    web_sys::window()\n        .and_then(|window| window.document())\n        .expect(\"document should exist\")\n}\n\nfn container() -> web_sys::HtmlElement {\n    let element = document()\n        .create_element(\"div\")\n        .expect(\"container should be created\");\n\n    document()\n        .body()\n        .expect(\"body should exist\")\n        .append_child(&element)\n        .expect(\"container should attach\");\n\n    element\n        .dyn_into::<web_sys::HtmlElement>()\n        .expect(\"container should be an HtmlElement\")\n}\n\nasync fn animation_frame_turn() {\n    let promise = js_sys::Promise::new(&mut |resolve, _reject| {\n        let callback = wasm_bindgen::closure::Closure::once_into_js({\n            let resolve = resolve.clone();\n            move || {\n                drop(resolve.call0(&wasm_bindgen::JsValue::UNDEFINED));\n            }\n        });\n\n        web_sys::window()\n            .expect(\"window should exist\")\n            .request_animation_frame(callback.unchecked_ref())\n            .expect(\"requestAnimationFrame should succeed\");\n    });\n\n    drop(wasm_bindgen_futures::JsFuture::from(promise).await);\n}\n\n#[component]\nfn NonceTrigger() -> Element {\n    let nonce_context = try_use_context::<ars_dioxus::ArsNonceCssCtx>()\n        .expect(\"ArsProvider should publish nonce context\");\n\n    ars_dioxus::append_nonce_css(String::from(\".probe { color: red; }\"));\n\n    rsx! {\n        span { \"data-testid\": \"nonce-rules-count\", \"{nonce_context.rules.read().len()}\" }\n    }\n}\n\nfn nonce_app() -> Element {\n    rsx! {\n        ArsProvider {\n            style_strategy: StyleStrategy::Nonce(String::from(\"dom-nonce\")),\n            NonceTrigger {}\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[wasm_bindgen_test(async)]\nasync fn ars_provider_with_nonce_strategy_auto_renders_style_tag_in_dom_on_wasm() {\n    let parent = container();\n\n    let dom = VirtualDom::new(nonce_app);\n\n    dioxus_web::launch::launch_virtual_dom(\n        dom,\n        dioxus_web::Config::new().rootelement(parent.clone().into()),\n    );\n\n    animation_frame_turn().await;\n    animation_frame_turn().await;\n\n    let style = parent\n        .query_selector(\"style[nonce='dom-nonce']\")\n        .expect(\"selector should be valid\")\n        .expect(\"nonce-bearing style tag should be in the DOM\");\n\n    let css = style\n        .text_content()\n        .expect(\"style tag should have CSS text\");\n\n    assert!(\n        css.contains(\".probe { color: red; }\"),\n        \"appended CSS rule should land in the nonce style tag on wasm: {css}\"\n    );\n\n    let wrapper = parent\n        .query_selector(\"[dir='ltr']\")\n        .expect(\"selector should be valid\")\n        .expect(\"provider wrapper should exist\");\n\n    assert_eq!(wrapper.get_attribute(\"dir\").as_deref(), Some(\"ltr\"));\n\n    parent.remove();\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Props)]\nstruct PlatformIdProbeProps {\n    outputs: Rc<RefCell<Vec<String>>>,\n}\n\nimpl PartialEq for PlatformIdProbeProps {\n    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {\n        Rc::ptr_eq(&self.outputs, &other.outputs)\n    }\n}\n\n#[expect(non_snake_case, reason = \"Dioxus components use PascalCase names\")]\nfn PlatformIdProbe(props: PlatformIdProbeProps) -> Element {\n    let platform = use_platform();\n\n    props.outputs.borrow_mut().push(platform.new_id());\n\n    rsx! {\n        div { \"data-testid\": \"platform-id-probe\" }\n    }\n}\n\n#[wasm_bindgen_test]\nfn use_platform_falls_back_to_web_platform_without_provider_on_wasm() {\n    let outputs = Rc::new(RefCell::new(Vec::<String>::new()));\n\n    fn app(outputs: Rc<RefCell<Vec<String>>>) -> Element {\n        rsx! {\n            PlatformIdProbe { outputs }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let mut dom = VirtualDom::new_with_props(app, Rc::clone(&outputs));\n\n    dom.rebuild_in_place();\n\n    let ids = outputs.borrow();\n\n    assert_eq!(ids.len(), 1, \"PlatformIdProbe should have run once\");\n\n    let id = &ids[0];\n\n    assert!(\n        !id.starts_with(\"null-id-\"),\n        \"on wasm32 + feature=\\\"web\\\", use_platform without provider must \\\n         resolve to WebPlatform (UUID), not NullPlatform: got {id}\"\n    );\n\n    // `WebPlatform::new_id()` delegates to `crypto.randomUUID()`, which\n    // produces strings of the form `xxxxxxxx-xxxx-Mxxx-Nxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx`\n    // (36 chars with four hyphens). Spot-check the shape so the oracle\n    // catches future regressions where the fallback chain reorders.\n    assert_eq!(\n        id.len(),\n        36,\n        \"WebPlatform::new_id() should return a UUID (got {id})\"\n    );\n    assert_eq!(\n        id.chars().filter(|c| *c == '-').count(),\n        4,\n        \"UUID v4 has 4 hyphen separators (got {id})\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Props)]\nstruct ContextPlatformProbeProps {\n    outputs: Rc<RefCell<Vec<String>>>,\n}\n\nimpl PartialEq for ContextPlatformProbeProps {\n    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {\n        Rc::ptr_eq(&self.outputs, &other.outputs)\n    }\n}\n\n#[expect(non_snake_case, reason = \"Dioxus components use PascalCase names\")]\nfn ContextPlatformProbe(props: ContextPlatformProbeProps) -> Element {\n    let context = try_use_context::<ArsContext>().expect(\"ArsProvider should publish ArsContext\");\n\n    props\n        .outputs\n        .borrow_mut()\n        .push(context.dioxus_platform.new_id());\n\n    rsx! {\n        div {}\n    }\n}\n\n#[wasm_bindgen_test]\nfn ars_provider_publishes_default_dioxus_platform_when_prop_absent_on_wasm() {\n    let outputs = Rc::new(RefCell::new(Vec::<String>::new()));\n\n    fn app(outputs: Rc<RefCell<Vec<String>>>) -> Element {\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider { ContextPlatformProbe { outputs } }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let mut dom = VirtualDom::new_with_props(app, Rc::clone(&outputs));\n\n    dom.rebuild_in_place();\n\n    let ids = outputs.borrow();\n\n    assert_eq!(ids.len(), 1, \"ContextPlatformProbe should have run once\");\n\n    let context_id = &ids[0];\n\n    // Sanity-check by allocating a fresh `default_dioxus_platform()` and\n    // confirming both produce IDs in the same family. On wasm32 + web that\n    // family is UUID (36 chars, 4 hyphens). This pins the §29 oracle:\n    // \"use_platform() returns the feature-flag-appropriate impl\".\n    let direct_id = default_dioxus_platform().new_id();\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        context_id.len(),\n        direct_id.len(),\n        \"context-resolved platform and direct default should produce \\\n         IDs of the same family (context={context_id} direct={direct_id})\"\n    );\n    assert_eq!(\n        context_id.chars().filter(|c| *c == '-').count(),\n        direct_id.chars().filter(|c| *c == '-').count(),\n        \"same hyphen count proves the same platform family\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        !context_id.starts_with(\"null-id-\"),\n        \"feature=\\\"web\\\" on wasm32 must publish WebPlatform, not NullPlatform\"\n    );\n}\n"
}

1	---
2	component: ArsProvider
3	category: utility
4	tier: stateless
5	foundation_deps: [architecture, accessibility]
6	shared_deps: []
7	related: []
8	references:
9	    ark-ui: Environment
10	---
11	
12	# ArsProvider
13	
14	`ArsProvider` is the **single root provider** for the ars-ui library. It supplies shared configuration, platform capabilities, provider-scoped modality state, i18n resources, and style strategy to all descendant components. It MUST be rendered at (or near) the application root.
15	
16	`ArsProvider` subsumes the formerly separate `LocaleProvider`, `PlatformEffectsProvider`, `IntlBackend`, `I18nProvider`, and `ArsStyleProvider`.
17	
18	## 1. API
19	
20	### 1.1 Props
21	
22	```rust
23	/// Props for the `ArsProvider` component.
24	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
25	pub struct Props {
26	    /// The active locale for i18n message formatting and text direction inference.
27	    /// Defaults to `Locale::parse("en-US").expect("en-US is always valid")`.
28	    pub locale: Option<Locale>,
29	
30	    /// Explicit reading direction override. When `None`, direction is inferred
31	    /// from `locale`. See `04-internationalization.md` §3.1 for `Direction`.
32	    pub direction: Option<Direction>,
33	
34	    /// Active color mode for theme-aware rendering. Defaults to `ColorMode::System`.
35	    pub color_mode: Option<ColorMode>,
36	
37	    /// When `true`, all descendant interactive components render as disabled.
38	    /// Defaults to `false`.
39	    pub disabled: Option<bool>,
40	
41	    /// When `true`, all descendant form fields render as read-only.
42	    /// Defaults to `false`.
43	    pub read_only: Option<bool>,
44	
45	    /// Optional prefix prepended to all generated IDs (for micro-frontend isolation).
46	    pub id_prefix: Option<String>,
47	
48	    /// ID of the container element for portal mounts.
49	    /// `None` means the platform default (e.g., `document.body` on web).
50	    pub portal_container_id: Option<String>,
51	
52	    /// ID of the root node for focus scope and portal queries.
53	    /// `None` means the platform default.
54	    pub root_node_id: Option<String>,
55	
56	    /// Platform capabilities for side effects (focus, timers, scroll-lock, positioning).
57	    /// Adapters provide platform-specific implementations (e.g., `WebPlatformEffects`).
58	    /// Defaults to `NullPlatformEffects` (no-op) for tests and SSR.
59	    pub platform: Option<Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>>,
60	
61	    /// Shared input-modality state for this provider root.
62	    /// Defaults to `DefaultModalityContext`.
63	    pub modality: Option<Arc<dyn ModalityContext>>,
64	
65	    /// Calendar/locale data provider for date-time components.
66	    /// Production uses `Icu4xBackend`; tests use `StubIntlBackend`.
67	    /// Defaults to `StubIntlBackend` (English-only).
68	    pub intl_backend: Option<Arc<dyn IntlBackend>>,
69	
70	    /// Per-component translation message registries.
71	    /// Defaults to empty (components use built-in English defaults).
72	    pub i18n_registries: Option<Rc<I18nRegistries>>,
73	
74	    /// CSS style injection strategy for all descendant ars components.
75	    /// Defaults to `StyleStrategy::Inline`.
76	    pub style_strategy: Option<StyleStrategy>,
77	}
78	```
79	
80	### 1.2 Connect / API
81	
82	`ArsProvider` is a context-only provider — it has no `Part` enum, no `ConnectApi`, and no `AttrMap` output. It publishes an `ArsContext` via the framework context system.
83	
84	```rust
85	/// Provided via framework context (Leptos provide_context / Dioxus use_context_provider).
86	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
87	pub struct ArsContext {
88	    locale: Locale,
89	    direction: Direction,
90	    color_mode: ColorMode,
91	    disabled: bool,
92	    read_only: bool,
93	    id_prefix: Option<String>,
94	    portal_container_id: Option<String>,
95	    root_node_id: Option<String>,
96	    platform: Arc<dyn PlatformEffects>,
97	    modality: Arc<dyn ModalityContext>,
98	    intl_backend: Arc<dyn IntlBackend>,
99	    i18n_registries: Arc<I18nRegistries>,
100	    style_strategy: StyleStrategy,
101	}
102	
103	impl ArsContext {
104	    /// Returns the active locale.
105	    pub fn locale(&self) -> &Locale { &self.locale }
106	    /// Returns the reading direction (explicit or locale-inferred).
107	    pub fn direction(&self) -> Direction { self.direction }
108	    /// Returns the active color mode.
109	    pub fn color_mode(&self) -> ColorMode { self.color_mode }
110	    /// Returns `true` when all descendant components should be disabled.
111	    pub fn disabled(&self) -> bool { self.disabled }
112	    /// Returns `true` when all descendant form fields should be read-only.
113	    pub fn read_only(&self) -> bool { self.read_only }
114	    /// Returns the optional ID prefix for generated IDs.
115	    pub fn id_prefix(&self) -> Option<&str> { self.id_prefix.as_deref() }
116	    /// Returns the portal container element ID, if set.
117	    pub fn portal_container_id(&self) -> Option<&str> { self.portal_container_id.as_deref() }
118	    /// Returns the root node ID for focus/portal scoping, if set.
119	    pub fn root_node_id(&self) -> Option<&str> { self.root_node_id.as_deref() }
120	    /// Returns the platform capabilities trait object.
121	    pub fn platform(&self) -> Arc<dyn PlatformEffects> { Arc::clone(&self.platform) }
122	    /// Returns the provider-scoped modality context.
123	    pub fn modality(&self) -> Arc<dyn ModalityContext> { Arc::clone(&self.modality) }
124	    /// Returns the ICU calendar/locale data provider.
125	    pub fn intl_backend(&self) -> Arc<dyn IntlBackend> { Arc::clone(&self.intl_backend) }
126	    /// Returns the i18n translation registries.
127	    pub fn i18n_registries(&self) -> &I18nRegistries { &self.i18n_registries }
128	    /// Returns the active CSS style injection strategy.
129	    pub fn style_strategy(&self) -> &StyleStrategy { &self.style_strategy }
130	}
131	
132	impl Default for ArsContext {
133	    fn default() -> Self {
134	        Self {
135	            locale: Locale::parse("en-US").expect("en-US is always valid"),
136	            direction: Direction::Ltr,
137	            color_mode: ColorMode::System,
138	            disabled: false,
139	            read_only: false,
140	            id_prefix: None,
141	            portal_container_id: None,
142	            root_node_id: None,
143	            platform: Arc::new(NullPlatformEffects),
144	            modality: Arc::new(DefaultModalityContext::new()),
145	            intl_backend: Arc::new(StubIntlBackend),
146	            i18n_registries: Rc::new(I18nRegistries::new()),
147	            style_strategy: StyleStrategy::Inline,
148	        }
149	    }
150	}
151	```
152	
153	Consumers access the context via the framework's context API:
154	
155	```rust,no_check
156	// Leptos
157	let ctx = use_context::<ArsContext>();
158	
159	// Dioxus
160	let ctx = try_use_context::<ArsContext>();
161	```
162	
163	Convenience hooks read from `ArsContext` with fallback defaults:
164	
165	- `use_locale()` — locale, falls back to `en-US`
166	- `use_number_formatter()` — locale-aware number formatting derived from the active `ArsProvider` locale
167	- `use_platform_effects()` — platform capabilities
168	- `use_modality_context()` — provider-scoped input-modality state
169	- `use_intl_backend()` — calendar/locale data
170	- `use_style_strategy()` — CSS style strategy, falls back to `Inline`
171	- `resolve_messages::<M>()` — translation registries
172	
173	> **Note:** `ArsContext` implements `Default` with sensible fallbacks: locale `en-US`, direction `Ltr`, color mode `System`, disabled `false`, read-only `false`, style strategy `Inline`.
174	
175	## 2. Anatomy
176	
177	```text
178	ArsProvider
179	└── Root <div>   dir="{direction}"
180	    └── {children}
181	```
182	
183	`ArsProvider` renders a single `<div dir="{direction}">` wrapper to propagate reading direction to descendants. It is otherwise a logical wrapper — no additional DOM structure.
184	
185	## 3. Accessibility
186	
187	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
188	
189	`ArsProvider` has no ARIA semantics. It is invisible to assistive technology. The `dir` attribute on the wrapper propagates reading direction for correct BiDi layout.
190	
191	## 4. Internal Usage
192	
193	| Consumer                 | What it reads                                                                                      |
194	| ------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
195	| `Portal`                 | `portal_container_id` — where to mount portal nodes                                                |
196	| `FocusScope`             | `root_node_id` — boundary for focus containment queries                                            |
197	| All i18n-dependent comps | `locale`, `direction` — for message formatting and layout                                          |
198	| ID generation (`use_id`) | `id_prefix` — prefix for micro-frontend isolation                                                  |
199	| Form field components    | `disabled`, `read_only` — cascade to descendant interactive components                             |
200	| Theme-aware components   | `color_mode` — for conditional rendering based on active color mode                                |
201	| All effect closures      | `platform` — via `use_platform_effects()` for focus, timers, scroll-lock, positioning, DOM queries |
202	| Focus / Hover / Press    | `modality` — via `use_modality_context()` for shared modality and global press state               |
203	| Date-time components     | `intl_backend` — via `use_intl_backend()` for calendar data (weekday names, month names, etc.)     |
204	| Numeric components       | `locale` — via `use_number_formatter()` for provider-derived number formatting                     |
205	| All stateful components  | `i18n_registries` — via `resolve_messages::<M>()` for per-component translation lookups            |
206	| All rendered components  | `style_strategy` — via `use_style_strategy()` for CSS injection method                             |
207	
208	## 5. Library Parity
209	
210	> Compared against: Ark UI (`Environment`).
211	
212	Ark UI's `Environment` component provides `getRootNode()` for Shadow DOM and iframe scoping. ars-ui's `ArsProvider` serves a broader purpose — it is the single root provider covering locale, direction, color mode, accessibility cascades, ID prefixing, portal/focus scoping, platform capabilities, i18n, and style strategy. The concepts overlap only in portal/focus scoping.
213	
214	| Feature                  | ars-ui                | Ark UI        | Notes                                    |
215	| ------------------------ | --------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------- |
216	| Portal container scoping | `portal_container_id` | (getRootNode) | Both scope portal mounts                 |
217	| Focus scope root         | `root_node_id`        | (getRootNode) | Both scope focus containment             |
218	| Locale / direction       | Yes                   | --            | ars-ui addition                          |
219	| Color mode               | Yes                   | --            | ars-ui addition                          |
220	| Disabled / read-only     | Yes                   | --            | ars-ui addition                          |
221	| ID prefix                | Yes                   | --            | ars-ui addition                          |
222	| Platform effects         | Yes                   | --            | ars-ui addition                          |
223	| ICU / i18n registries    | Yes                   | --            | ars-ui addition                          |
224	| Style strategy (CSP)     | Yes                   | --            | ars-ui addition                          |
225	| Context-only provider    | Yes                   | Yes           | Both render no significant DOM structure |
226	
227	**Gaps:** None. ars-ui is a superset.
228	
```

spec/leptos-components/utility/ars-provider.md:56:The adapter publishes `ArsContext` with `provide_context`. It also publishes an `ArsNonceCssCtx` collector owned by the provider instance and automatically renders `ArsNonceStyle` when `style_strategy` is `StyleStrategy::Nonce(nonce)`. Consumers use `use_context::<ArsContext>()`. The convenience hooks `use_locale()` and `use_direction()` read from this context with fallback defaults.
spec/leptos-components/utility/ars-provider.md:174:| `use_direction()` hook      | required  | Read direction from ArsContext with Ltr fallback | all direction-dependent components          | Parallel to `use_locale()`              |
spec/dioxus-components/utility/ars-provider.md:80:The adapter publishes `ArsContext` with `use_context_provider`. It also publishes an `ArsNonceCssCtx` collector owned by the provider instance and automatically renders `ArsNonceStyle` when `style_strategy` is `StyleStrategy::Nonce(nonce)`. Consumers use `try_use_context::<ArsContext>()`. The convenience hooks `use_locale()` and `use_direction()` read from this context with fallback defaults. During Dioxus SSR, all context values are platform-agnostic and require no browser globals.

368	/// Returns the current locale signal from provider context.
369	#[must_use]
370	pub fn use_locale() -> Signal<Locale> {
371	    let fallback = use_signal(locales::en_us);
372	
373	    try_use_context::<ArsContext>().map_or_else(
374	        || {
375	            warn_missing_provider("use_locale");

378	        },

383	/// Resolves the effective locale for an adapter component instance.
384	///
385	/// Returns `adapter_props_locale.cloned()` when set, otherwise reads the
386	/// surrounding [`ArsProvider`](ArsContext) locale via [`use_locale`] and
387	/// clones it. Subscribes the calling component to locale changes (via
388	/// `Signal::read`), so locale-dependent output stays reactive.
389	///
390	/// This mirrors the Leptos
391	/// [`resolve_locale`](ars_leptos::resolve_locale) utility — adapter
392	/// component authors should reach for this helper instead of
393	/// hand-rolling the `Option` + provider fallback chain.
394	#[must_use]
395	pub fn resolve_locale(adapter_props_locale: Option<&Locale>) -> Locale {
396	    adapter_props_locale
397	        .cloned()
398	        .unwrap_or_else(|| use_locale().read().clone())
399	}
400	
401	/// Resolves a memoized number formatter from the current provider locale.
402	#[must_use]
403	pub fn use_number_formatter<F>(options: F) -> Memo<number::Formatter>
404	where
405	    F: Fn() -> number::FormatOptions + 'static,
406	{
407	    use_resolved_number_formatter(None, options)
408	}
409

412	pub(crate) fn use_resolved_number_formatter<F>(

199	## 22. Shared Adapter Helper Notes
200	
201	| Helper concept      | Required? | Responsibility                                  | Reused by                       | Notes                                   |
202	| ------------------- | --------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- |
203	| `use_locale()` hook | required  | Read locale from ArsContext with en-US fallback | all locale-dependent components | Defined in `09-adapter-dioxus.md` §16.1 |
204	
205	## 23. Framework-Specific Behavior
206	
207	Dioxus targets web, desktop, and mobile. `ArsContext` bundles platform-agnostic configuration, the `PlatformEffects` trait object, and the Dioxus-specific `DioxusPlatform` trait object. The `dioxus_platform` prop is an adapter-only extra with no core equivalent — it provides Dioxus-specific platform services (file pickers, clipboard, drag data) not covered by `PlatformEffects`. The Dioxus adapter resolves the platform implementation via feature flags: `WebPlatform` (web), `DesktopPlatform` (desktop), `NullPlatform` (SSR/tests/mobile fallback). The `style_strategy` field controls CSS injection for all descendant ars components.
208	
```

{

  "old_string": "#[component]\nfn PlatformProbe() -> Element {\n    let context = try_use_context::<ArsContext>().expect(\"ArsProvider should publish ArsContext\");\n\n    let id = context.dioxus_platform.new_id();\n\n    rsx! {\n        span { \"data-testid\": \"platform-id\", \"{id}\" }\n    }\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_resolves_dioxus_platform_default_via_feature_flag_when_prop_absent() {\n    fn app() -> Element {\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider { PlatformProbe {} }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let html = render_app(app);\n\n    // SSR runs on the native target without `web` or `desktop` features, so\n    // `default_dioxus_platform()` resolves to `NullPlatform`, whose `new_id()`\n    // produces strings prefixed with `null-id-`. This pins the §29 oracle that\n    // `use_platform()` returns the feature-flag-appropriate impl.\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"data-testid=\"platform-id\"\"#),\n        \"PlatformProbe should render inside the provider: {html}\"\n    );\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(\"null-id-\"),\n        \"default DioxusPlatform on native (no web/desktop) is NullPlatform; \\\n         its new_id() output should appear in the rendered HTML: {html}\"\n    );\n}",
  "new_string": "#[component]\nfn PlatformProbe() -> Element {\n    let context = try_use_context::<ArsContext>().expect(\"ArsProvider should publish ArsContext\");\n\n    let context_id = context.dioxus_platform.new_id();\n    let direct_id = default_dioxus_platform().new_id();\n    let context_id_len = context_id.len();\n    let direct_id_len = direct_id.len();\n    let context_id_hyphens = context_id.chars().filter(|c| *c == '-').count();\n    let direct_id_hyphens = direct_id.chars().filter(|c| *c == '-').count();\n\n    rsx! {\n        span { \"data-testid\": \"context-id\", \"{context_id}\" }\n        span { \"data-testid\": \"direct-id\", \"{direct_id}\" }\n        span { \"data-testid\": \"context-id-len\", \"{context_id_len}\" }\n        span { \"data-testid\": \"direct-id-len\", \"{direct_id_len}\" }\n        span { \"data-testid\": \"context-id-hyphens\", \"{context_id_hyphens}\" }\n        span { \"data-testid\": \"direct-id-hyphens\", \"{direct_id_hyphens}\" }\n    }\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn ars_provider_resolves_dioxus_platform_default_via_feature_flag_when_prop_absent() {\n    fn app() -> Element {\n        rsx! {\n            ArsProvider { PlatformProbe {} }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let html = render_app(app);\n\n    // Spec §29 oracle: \"`use_platform()` returns the feature-flag-appropriate\n    // impl without explicit prop.\" Test is feature-flag-agnostic — the context\n    // platform must produce IDs in the same family (same length, same hyphen\n    // count) as a freshly-constructed `default_dioxus_platform()`. On native\n    // SSR without `desktop`, both produce `null-id-N`. With `--all-features`\n    // active, `desktop` engages and both produce v4 UUIDs.\n    assert!(\n        html.contains(r#\"data-testid=\"context-id\"\"#),\n        \"PlatformProbe should render inside the provider: {html}\"\n    );\n\n    // Extract the rendered length values from the HTML; both must agree.\n    // We just spot-check the equality fingerprint pair is present.\n    let context_len = html\n        .split(r#\"data-testid=\"context-id-len\">\"#)\n        .nth(1)\n        .and_then(|tail| tail.split('<').next())\n        .expect(\"context-id-len span should be present\");\n    let direct_len = html\n        .split(r#\"data-testid=\"direct-id-len\">\"#)\n        .nth(1)\n        .and_then(|tail| tail.split('<').next())\n        .expect(\"direct-id-len span should be present\");\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        context_len, direct_len,\n        \"context-resolved platform and direct default_dioxus_platform() \\\n         should produce IDs of the same length (proves same impl family): {html}\"\n    );\n\n    let context_hyphens = html\n        .split(r#\"data-testid=\"context-id-hyphens\">\"#)\n        .nth(1)\n        .and_then(|tail| tail.split('<').next())\n        .expect(\"context-id-hyphens span should be present\");\n    let direct_hyphens = html\n        .split(r#\"data-testid=\"direct-id-hyphens\">\"#)\n        .nth(1)\n        .and_then(|tail| tail.split('<').next())\n        .expect(\"direct-id-hyphens span should be present\");\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        context_hyphens, direct_hyphens,\n        \"context-resolved platform and direct default_dioxus_platform() \\\n         should produce IDs with the same hyphen count: {html}\"\n    );\n}"
}
