> AGENT

Here's the final result for your review before commit:

## Summary

Closes **#347** (Leptos ArsProvider adapter) and **#477** (Dioxus ArsProvider adapter — reopened, was prematurely closed).

### Changes

**New integration test files (4 files, 940 lines):**
- `crates/ars-leptos/tests/ars_provider.rs` — 7 SSR tests (`view!{}.to_html()` oracles for `<div dir>`, RTL inference, explicit direction override, nonce/inline/cssom strategy emission, color-mode/disabled/read-only wrapper hygiene).
- `crates/ars-leptos/tests/ars_provider_wasm.rs` — 3 wasm tests via `mount_to` + real DOM probes (all-props round-trip + dogfooded `use_direction()`, nonce style tag DOM presence, `use_style_strategy()` Inline fallback).
- `crates/ars-dioxus/tests/ars_provider.rs` — 9 SSR tests via `dioxus_ssr::render` (same coverage + feature-flag-agnostic `dioxus_platform` default + `ArsProviderProps` `PartialEq` smoke test).
- `crates/ars-dioxus/tests/ars_provider_wasm.rs` — 3 wasm tests via `dioxus_web::launch::launch_virtual_dom` for real-DOM nonce style tag + `use_platform()`/context platform UUID verification.

**Preludes (symmetric additions):**
- `crates/ars-leptos/src/prelude.rs`: re-exports `ArsProvider`, `ars_core::ColorMode`.
- `crates/ars-dioxus/src/prelude.rs`: re-exports `ArsProvider`, `ArsProviderProps`, `ars_core::ColorMode`.

**Audit findings landed in same PR:**
- Added `use_direction()` helper to both adapters (Leptos spec §22 listed it as required; impl was missing). With Ltr fallback, matches `use_locale` pattern. Unit tests inline + dogfooded in Leptos wasm probe.
- Updated Dioxus spec §22 to list `use_direction()` and `use_style_strategy()` for parity with Leptos spec §22.
- Added `use_direction()` test scenarios to both specs §28/§29/§30.

**Verification:** All 7+3+9+3 = 22 new tests pass; both inline unit tests for `use_direction()` pass; `cargo xci-fast` all 10 gates green; clippy `-D warnings` clean on both adapter crates; `cargo xtask spec validate` + spec snippet compile both pass.

### Plan notes already addressed
- Issue templates listed "ARIA attributes from core ConnectApi" and "Axe-core audit" — **not applicable** for context-only `ArsProvider` (no ConnectApi, no `data-ars-scope`, no ARIA semantics per spec §1.2 / §3.1).
- `ArsContext` deliberately stays out of the prelude (published context value, not a configuration input).

May I commit, push, and open the PR? (Will run the `waiting-for-codex-review` skill after first push.)

Yes — all 6 widget examples use `ars_*::{ArsProvider, prelude::t}` because `ArsProvider` wasn't in the prelude before. Now that it is, the natural simplification is `ars_*::prelude::{ArsProvider, t}`. This is a Phase 2 audit gap I missed — landing in the same PR.

Final scope: 19 files total. Let me ask for commit approval before pushing.

Per CLAUDE.md step 8, presenting for your review before commit:

**Files changed:**
- **New test files (4):** `crates/ars-{leptos,dioxus}/tests/ars_provider{,_wasm}.rs` — 22 integration tests total, all passing.
- **Adapter source (6):** `crates/ars-{leptos,dioxus}/src/{lib,prelude,provider}.rs` — adds `use_direction()` helper + prelude exports.
- **Spec docs (2):** `spec/{leptos,dioxus}-components/utility/ars-provider.md` — added `use_direction` to §22/§28/§29/§30 for parity (Dioxus also gained `use_style_strategy` row in §22).
- **Widgets examples (6):** `examples/widgets-{leptos,dioxus}{,-css,-tailwind}/src/main.rs` — simplified `use ars_*::{ArsProvider, prelude::t}` to `use ars_*::prelude::{ArsProvider, t}` (validates new prelude export).

**Audit summary (landed in this PR):**
- Phase 1 (spec drift): Added `use_direction()` to both adapters (Leptos spec §22 required it).
- Phase 2 (5 rounds, then 2 clean): Updated Dioxus spec §22 + both specs §28/§29/§30 + dogfooded `use_direction()` in Leptos wasm probe.
- Phase 3 (coverage): provider.rs at 98%/94% line coverage, clippy `-D warnings` clean, all required test types present.

**Verification:** `cargo xci-fast` all 10 gates green; all 7+3+9+3 integration tests pass; both inline `use_direction` unit tests pass; `cargo xtask spec validate` + spec snippet compile clean; widgets examples compile clean.

May I proceed with `git add -A`, commit, push, and `gh pr create` with `Closes #347` and `Closes #477`? Then I'll invoke `waiting-for-codex-review`.

{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"Finish ArsProvider Leptos and Dioxus adapters\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\n- Add adapter-tier integration tests, prelude exposure, and a missing `use_direction()` helper required by Leptos spec §22 for the `ArsProvider` component shell in both `ars-leptos` and `ars-dioxus`.\n- The provider component itself and ~40 inline unit/wasm tests already shipped via #190/#191/#193/#194, but the discrete adapter-component deliverable (dedicated `tests/ars_provider*.rs`, prelude export, full spec §28 oracle coverage) was never completed; #477 was closed prematurely as superseded.\n- Each adapter now publishes `ArsProvider` (and Dioxus `ArsProviderProps`) plus `ars_core::ColorMode` through its prelude. The six widgets examples switched from `ars_*::{ArsProvider, prelude::t}` to `ars_*::prelude::{ArsProvider, t}` to validate and dogfood the new export path.\n\n## What landed\n\n- **New integration tests (4 files, 22 tests):**\n  - `crates/ars-leptos/tests/ars_provider.rs` — 7 SSR oracles via `view!{}.to_html()` (default `dir=\"ltr\"`, RTL locale inference, explicit direction override, `StyleStrategy::{Nonce,Inline,Cssom}` emission, wrapper attr hygiene).\n  - `crates/ars-leptos/tests/ars_provider_wasm.rs` — 3 wasm tests via `mount_to` + real DOM probes (all-props round-trip dogfooding `use_direction()`, nonce `<style>` tag presence, `use_style_strategy()` fallback to Inline).\n  - `crates/ars-dioxus/tests/ars_provider.rs` — 9 SSR oracles via `dioxus_ssr::render` (mirror Leptos coverage + feature-flag-agnostic `dioxus_platform` default fingerprint + `ArsProviderProps: Clone + PartialEq` smoke test).\n  - `crates/ars-dioxus/tests/ars_provider_wasm.rs` — 3 wasm tests via `dioxus_web::launch::launch_virtual_dom` (real-DOM nonce `<style>`, `use_platform()` falls back to `WebPlatform` UUID outside provider, context-published `dioxus_platform` matches `default_dioxus_platform()` family).\n\n- **`use_direction()` helper added to both adapters.** Documented as `required` in Leptos spec §22 but the implementation was missing on both sides; mirrored from `use_locale()` with `Direction::Ltr` fallback when no `ArsProvider` is in scope. Inline unit tests cover the fallback and the published-context paths.\n\n- **Spec drift fixes** (caught by `post-implementation-audit`):\n  - Dioxus spec §22 (`Shared Adapter Helper Notes`) now lists `use_direction()` and `use_style_strategy()` for parity with the Leptos spec.\n  - Both adapter specs §28/§29/§30 gained `use_direction()` test scenarios, oracle row, and checklist item.\n\n- **Preludes (symmetric):**\n  - `ars-leptos::prelude` re-exports `ArsProvider` and `ars_core::ColorMode`.\n  - `ars-dioxus::prelude` re-exports `ArsProvider`, `ArsProviderProps`, and `ars_core::ColorMode`.\n\n- **Widgets examples** (`examples/widgets-{leptos,dioxus}{,-css,-tailwind}/src/main.rs`) now reach `ArsProvider` through the prelude — confirms the new exports flow end-to-end through the public consumption path.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [x] `cargo test -p ars-leptos --features ssr --test ars_provider` — 7/7 pass.\n- [x] `wasm-pack test --headless --chrome crates/ars-leptos --features csr --test ars_provider_wasm` — 3/3 pass.\n- [x] `cargo test -p ars-dioxus --test ars_provider` — 9/9 pass under default and `--all-features`.\n- [x] `wasm-pack test --headless --chrome crates/ars-dioxus --features web --test ars_provider_wasm` — 3/3 pass.\n- [x] Inline `use_direction` tests pass for both adapters.\n- [x] `cargo xci-fast` — all 10 gates green (fmt, check, clippy, unit, integration, adapter, spec-compile-snippets, error-variant-coverage, mutual-exclusion, snapshot-count).\n- [x] `cargo xtask spec validate` — 340 files clean.\n- [x] `cargo clippy -p ars-leptos -p ars-dioxus --all-features --tests -- -D warnings` clean.\n\n## Spec parity notes (not changing the spec contract)\n\n- Issue #347's \"Tests to add first\" boilerplate listed \"ARIA attributes from core ConnectApi\" and \"Axe-core audit using `data-ars-scope` selector\". Neither applies to `ArsProvider`: spec §1.2 documents it as a context-only provider with no `ConnectApi`/`Part`/`data-ars-scope`, and the `<div dir>` wrapper has no ARIA semantics (§3.1, §26). This PR delivers against the §28 test scenarios — the authoritative list.\n- `ArsContext` deliberately stays out of the prelude (published context value, not a configuration input). Advanced users still reach it via `ars_{leptos,dioxus}::ArsContext`.\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\"",

}

{
  "command": "prev=\"\"\nend_time=$(($(date +%s) + 540))\nwhile [ $(date +%s) -lt $end_time ]; do\n  reactions=$(gh api repos/fogodev/ars-ui/issues/661/reactions \\\n    --jq '.[] | \"\\(.user.login):\\(.content)\"' 2>/dev/null | sort)\n  threads=$(gh api graphql -f query='query($o:String!,$r:String!,$n:Int!){\n    repository(owner:$o,name:$r){pullRequest(number:$n){\n      reviewThreads(first:100){nodes{id isResolved comments(first:1){nodes{author{login}}}}}}}}' \\\n    -F o=fogodev -F r=ars-ui -F n=661 \\\n    --jq '.data.repository.pullRequest.reviewThreads.nodes\n          | map(select(.isResolved==false\n                       and (.comments.nodes[0].author.login | test(\"codex\"; \"i\"))))\n          | length' 2>/dev/null)\n\n  codex_eyes=$(echo \"$reactions\" | grep -E \"codex.*:eyes$\" || true)\n  codex_thumb=$(echo \"$reactions\" | grep -E \"codex.*:\\+1$\" || true)\n\n  cur=\"reactions=[$reactions] threads=$threads\"\n  if [ \"$cur\" != \"$prev\" ]; then\n    echo \"[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $cur\"\n  fi\n  prev=$cur\n\n  if [ -n \"$codex_thumb\" ]; then\n    echo \"VERDICT=approval\"\n    exit 0\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$codex_eyes\" ] && [ \"$threads\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    echo \"VERDICT=needs_action threads=$threads\"\n    exit 0\n  fi\n\n  sleep 60\ndone\necho \"TIMEOUT (re-invoke needed)\"",
  "description": "Foreground polling loop, 9 min budget",
  "timeout": 600000
}
